*Licheng Zhang,Bach Le,Pengtao Zhao,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Web UI图像变化描述任务（WUICC）及其首个数据集和基准测试WUICC-bench，用于解决视觉回归测试中像素级比较的局限性，旨在通过自然语言描述UI变化而非仅提供二进制标志或高亮区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉回归测试（VRT）广泛使用像素级比较方法，该方法语义盲目，将渲染噪声和真实缺陷同等对待，产生大量误报，迫使开发人员和测试人员在每个发布周期花费大量时间手动审查标记的差异。现有行业工具虽应用机器学习但缺乏公开评估，且缺乏支持自然语言描述UI变化的数据集或基准。

Method: 提出新任务Web UI图像变化描述（WUICC），位于VRT和图像差异描述（IDC）的交叉点。发布首个数据集和基准WUICC-bench。评估了11种代表性IDC方法和2种零样本通用大型语言模型。

Result: 研究发现：（1）现有方法在Web UI领域表现不佳，因其布局多样性、密集文本和细粒度变化；（2）然而经过训练的方法已比VRT依赖的像素级比较更能选择性抑制无意义的视觉噪声，为未来领域特定研究奠定坚实基础。

Conclusion: WUICC任务填补了视觉回归测试中自然语言描述UI变化的空白，WUICC-bench为相关研究提供了首个评估基准。虽然现有方法在Web UI领域面临挑战，但已展现出优于传统像素级比较的选择性噪声抑制能力，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Visual regression testing (VRT) is a standard quality assurance step in modern software release pipelines. On every change, it re-renders user interface (UI) screenshots, compares each one against an approved baseline image, and routes any detected difference to a human reviewer who decides whether it is an intended update or an unintended regression. A widely used approach, especially in open-source and continuous-integration pipelines, is pixel-level comparison, which is semantically blind and treats rendering noise and genuine defects identically, producing large volumes of false positives that force developers and testers to spend substantial time and effort manually reviewing flagged differences at every release cycle. Industry tools apply machine learning to VRT, but lack public evaluation. More critically, no dataset or benchmark exists to support natural language descriptions of UI changes, a capability that tells testers what changed in words instead of leaving them to interpret a binary flag or a highlighted region. To address the gap, we propose a new task, Web UI Image Change Captioning (WUICC), which sits at the intersection of VRT and image difference captioning (IDC), and release WUICC-bench, its first dataset and benchmark for the task. We evaluate eleven representative IDC methods, together with two zero-shot general-purpose LLMs. We find that: (1) these methods tend to struggle in the Web UI domain due to its layout diversity, dense text, and fine-grained changes, and (2) yet the trained methods already suppress non-meaningful visual noise far more selectively than the pixel-level comparison VRT relies on, providing a solid foundation for future domain-specific research.

</details>


### [37] [ReQuest: Rethinking-based Question-Aware Frame Selection for Long-Form Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01737)
*Minkuk Kim,Suyong Yun,Young Tae Kim,Jinyoung Moon,Jinwoo Choi,Seong Tae Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReQuest：一种基于不确定性的问题自适应关键帧选择管道，用于长视频问答，通过选择性计算提高效率，无需修改或微调底层MLLM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在视频理解方面取得了进步，但长视频问答在固定的输入令牌预算下面临挑战，均匀采样对证据定位效率低下，需要更有效的关键帧选择方法

Method: 1. 轻量级问题感知选择器（从MLLM生成的监督中蒸馏）
2. 再思考路由（当模型不确定时触发额外推理，使用长度自适应标准）
3. 不确定性引导的自适应非极大值抑制（选择时间多样化的帧，根据问题难度调整间隔）
作为即插即用方法，不修改底层MLLM

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU和LongVideoBench数据集上的实验显示，ReQuest实现了持续准确率提升，同时保持有竞争力的计算成本，在中长视频场景中表现尤为突出

Conclusion: ReQuest通过问题自适应关键帧选择和不确定性驱动计算，有效解决了长视频问答中的证据定位挑战，提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced video understanding, yet long-form video QA remains challenging under fixed input token budgets, where uniform sampling can be inefficient for evidence localization. We propose ReQuest , an uncertainty-driven, question-adaptive keyframe selection pipeline that aligns question intent with relevant video content through selective computation. ReQuest integrates (i) a lightweight question-aware selector distilled from MLLM-generated supervision, (ii) Re-thinking Routing that triggers additional inference only when the model is uncertain with a length-adaptive criterion, and (iii) uncertainty-guided adaptive non-maximum suppression that selects temporally diverse frames while adjusting spacing based on question difficulty. As a plug-andplay method, ReQuest improves long-video QA without modifying or fine-tuning the underlying MLLM. Experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench demonstrate consistent accuracy gains with competitive computational cost, with particularly strong improvements in medium and long video regimes.

</details>


### [38] [InterCMDM: Block-Causal Diffusion for Autoregressive Human Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01743)
*Qing Yu,Kent Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterCMDM：一种用于双人交互生成的块因果潜在扩散框架，通过双流因果扩散Transformer和注意力掩码统一多种协调行为，实现可控的长期序列生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限：交互扩散模型使用双向注意力掩盖因果性，阻碍流式生成；自回归方法存在时间漂移问题，导致协调退化。需要既能保持因果性又能稳定生成长期交互的方法。

Method: 1. 提出块因果潜在扩散框架InterCMDM；2. 设计双流因果扩散Transformer，为每个人维持单独的因果流，同时通过统一的双流注意力建模人际依赖；3. 使用多任务注意力掩码统一多种协调行为；4. 通过块级扩散目标实现稳定的潜在序列生成。

Result: 在InterHuman和Inter-X数据集上达到SOTA性能，在文本-动作对齐、真实性和长期连续性方面均有改进，支持同时动作、反应响应、领导-跟随动态和独立运动等多种协调行为。

Conclusion: InterCMDM通过统一的注意力机制实现了可控的双人交互生成，解决了现有方法在因果性保持和长期稳定性方面的问题，为文本条件下的交互生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-conditioned human interaction generation must capture both long-range temporal causality within each individual and tightly coupled coordination between partners. Existing interaction diffusion models typically denoise full sequences using bidirectional attention, which obscures causality and hinders streaming and long-horizon generation. Autoregressive alternatives enforce causality but often suffer from temporal drift, leading to coordination degradation and unstable interaction dynamics over time. We propose InterCMDM, a block-causal latent diffusion framework for autoregressive two-person interaction generation. InterCMDM introduces a Dual-Stream Causal Diffusion Transformer that maintains separate causal streams for each person while modeling inter-person dependencies via unified dual-stream attention with multi-task attention masks. These masks unify interaction modeling within a single attention mechanism and support diverse coordination behaviors, including simultaneous actions, reactive responses, leader-follower dynamics, and independent motion. By training a single model across these mask configurations as a form of data augmentation, InterCMDM enables controllable interaction generation by simply selecting the desired attention mask at inference time. Finally, a block-wise diffusion objective enables stable latent rollout over long sequences without repeated decode-encode cycles. InterCMDM achieves state-of-the-art performance on InterHuman and Inter-X, improving text-motion alignment, realism, and long-horizon continuity.

</details>


### [39] [RTE-FM-Dehazer: Radiative Transfer Equation Inspired Flow Matching for Real-World Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01748)
*Chenfeng Wei,Chun Wang,Boyang Zhao,Si Zuo,Shenhong Wang,Chenguang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RTE-FM-Dehazer去雾方法和P-HAZE数据集，通过辐射传输方程改进流匹配技术，无需ASM先验，在真实世界基准测试中取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单图像去雾方法面临两大限制：1) 过度依赖大气散射模型先验，但ASM的单次散射和均匀介质假设在实际场景中常被违反，导致残留雾气和颜色漂移；2) 缺乏大规模真实雾霾/清晰图像对，现有合成方法无法重现自然雾霾的复杂性。

Method: 提出了RTE-FM-Dehazer方法：1) 采用辐射传输方程替代ASM，能够同时考虑散射和吸收，适应非均匀、多次散射的真实雾霾场景；2) 利用RTE扩散-吸收项与流匹配中ODE的结构相似性，从简化的RTE推导出扩散-吸收正则化器，引导流匹配轨迹；3) 构建自动化流水线并发布P-HAZE数据集，包含5万对真实感雾霾/清晰图像对。

Result: 实验评估表明，仅在P-HAZE数据集上训练的RTE-FM-Dehazer能够有效消除残留雾气和颜色漂移等伪影，展现出强大的跨域泛化能力，在五个真实世界去雾基准测试中取得领先结果。

Conclusion: 通过结合辐射传输方程的物理约束和流匹配的生成能力，配合高质量合成数据集，能够显著提升单图像去雾的性能和鲁棒性，突破了传统基于ASM方法的局限性。

Abstract: Single-image dehazing aims to recover a clear scene from a hazy image and is generally formulated as an image-to-image translation task; however, it faces two limitations. Its performance depends heavily on the haze-formation priors embedded in the model. Prevailing methods adopt the Atmospheric Scattering Model (ASM), whose assumptions of single scattering and homogeneous media are often violated, leading to residual haze and color drift. Moreover, large-scale real hazy/clear pairs are impractical to collect, and existing synthesis approaches fail to reproduce the full complexity of natural haze. To address these issues, we present RTE-FM-Dehazer, a novel dehazing approach, together with a scalable data pipeline. Unlike the ASM, the Radiative Transfer Equation (RTE) jointly accounts for both scattering and absorption, naturally accommodating the non-homogeneous, multiple-scattering media that characterize real hazy scenes. Motivated by the structural similarity between the RTE diffusion-absorption term and the ODE in flow matching, we introduce a diffusion-absorption regularizer derived from a reduced RTE, to steer the flow matching trajectory at each step. Next, leveraging modern vision-language models, we build an automated pipeline and release P-HAZE, a dataset of 50000 realistic hazy/clear pairs. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that RTE-FM-Dehazer, trained solely on P-HAZE, effectively eliminates artifacts like residual haze and color drift, exhibits strong cross-domain generalization, and achieves leading results on five real-world dehazing benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [MedStreamBench: A Time-Aware Benchmark for Streaming and Proactive Medical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01751)
*Yuan Wang,Shujian Gao,Songtao Jiang,Zhengyu Hu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedStreamBench：一个用于时间感知医疗视频理解的新基准，评估模型不仅回答正确性，还包括回答时机、延迟判断和主动预警能力，填补了基准评估与临床部署需求之间的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗视频基准主要评估模型是否产生正确答案，但很少评估模型是否在正确时间回答。在真实临床环境中，AI系统必须不仅决定预测什么，还要决定何时回答、延迟判断或主动发出警报，这造成了基准评估与部署需求之间的关键差距。

Method: 提出了MedStreamBench基准，整合了22个医疗数据集和5,419个问答实例，涵盖四个时间设置：回顾性、当前、未来和主动。与传统基准假设完全视频访问不同，该基准限制模型使用时间有界的证据窗口，支持单轮和流式评估，并引入了主动监控设置，要求模型决定是否以及何时触发临床相关警报。

Result: 对领先的通用和医疗视觉语言模型的实验显示，离线识别与时间基础决策之间存在显著差距，在流式和主动设置中性能显著下降。除了答案正确性外，基准还通过响应性和后证据稳定性评估时间行为。

Conclusion: MedStreamBench填补了医疗视频理解中时间感知评估的关键空白，强调了模型不仅需要正确回答，还需要在适当的时间做出决策的重要性，这对于AI系统在真实临床环境中的有效部署至关重要。

Abstract: Existing medical video benchmarks primarily evaluate whether a model produces the correct answer, but rarely assess whether it answers at the right time. In real clinical settings, AI systems must decide not only what to predict, but also when to answer, defer judgment, or proactively raise alerts. This creates a critical gap between benchmark evaluation and deployment requirements. We present MedStreamBench, a benchmark for time-aware medical video understanding. MedStreamBench integrates 22 medical datasets and 5,419 QA instances across four temporal settings: retrospective, present, future, and proactive. Unlike conventional benchmarks that assume full-video access, MedStreamBench restricts models to temporally bounded evidence windows and supports both single-turn and streaming evaluation. We further introduce a proactive monitoring setting that requires models to determine whether and when clinically relevant alerts should be triggered. Beyond answer correctness, MedStreamBench evaluates temporal behavior through responsiveness and post-evidence stability. Experiments on leading general-purpose and medical vision-language models reveal a substantial gap between offline recognition and temporally grounded decision-making, with performance dropping markedly in streaming and proactive settings. Our benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Venn2024/MedStreamBench.

</details>


### [41] [The Turning Point of 3D Plant Phenotyping: 3D Foundation Models Enable Minute-to-Second Cross-Crop Reconstruction and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01753)
*Hanyue Jia,Wei Zhou,Wenbo Zhou,Yanan Li,Hao Lu,Tingting Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于3D基础模型(3DFMs)的跨作物3D植物表型分析框架，显著简化传统繁琐的3D植物表型分析流程，将平均重建时间从6.52分钟降低到1.58秒，同时保持高质量重建和表型分析精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D植物表型分析流程复杂、通量低，涉及多视角成像、脆弱的3D重建流程，以及从重建几何到表型提取的额外成本。在低成本数据采集（如智能手机视频或稀疏采样图像）中，这些问题更加严重，因为视图重叠有限且存在自遮挡。

Method: 提出基于3D基础模型的跨作物3D表型分析框架：1）用3DFM前馈几何恢复替代COLMAP式稀疏初始化；2）结合几何约束的3D高斯泼溅进行密集重建；3）通过迭代视图合成和精炼实现少视图重建；4）通过2D到3D语义转换、度量尺度恢复和器官实例分离将重建几何转换为可测量的器官。构建了包含智能手机采集图像、多样化植物形态和手动标注的跨作物数据集。

Result: 在26个植物序列上的实验表明，3D基础模型将平均重建时间从6.52分钟降低到1.58秒（约250倍加速），同时保持高质量重建和表型分析精度。框架支持低成本图像采集到快速重建、感知、尺度恢复和表型测量的全流程。

Conclusion: 3D基础模型为高通量3D植物表型分析提供了一条新颖的技术路径，能够显著简化传统复杂流程，实现从低成本图像采集到快速重建、感知和表型测量的高效流程，为植物表型分析领域带来革命性改进。

Abstract: 3D plant phenotyping is notoriously known to be procedure-complicated and of low throughput due to the extensive multi-view imaging, the fragile 3D reconstruction pipeline, and the additional cost from reconstructed geometry to phenotypic extraction. These limitations are further amplified in low-cost data acquisition, where smartphone videos or sparsely sampled multi-view images provide limited view overlap and self-occlusion. In this work, we show that the conventional 3D plant phenotyping pipeline could be streamlined and significantly accelerated with 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs), and particularly, present one of the first cross-crop 3D phenotyping frameworks powered by 3DFMs. The framework replaces COLMAP-style sparse initialization with 3DFM-based feed-forward geometric recovery, combines geometry-constrained 3D Gaussian Splatting for dense reconstruction, enables few-view reconstruction through iterative view synthesis and refinement, and converts reconstructed geometry into measurable organs through 2D-to-3D semantic transfer, metric scale recovery, and organ instance separation. We further construct a cross-crop dataset with smartphone-based image acquisition, diverse plant morphologies, and manual annotations for segmentation and phenotypic evaluation. Experiments across 26 plant sequences show that 3D Foundation Models reduce the average reconstruction time from 6.52 minutes to 1.58 seconds while maintaining high reconstruction quality and phenotyping accuracy. These results suggest a fresh technical route for high-throughput 3D plant phenotyping, from low-cost image acquisition to fast reconstruction, perception, scale recovery, and phenotypic measurement.

</details>


### [42] [ProSAC-CT: Progressive Spectral-Anatomical Co-Guided Multi-Stage Diffusion Model for Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01756)
*Xuepeng Liu,Zetong Liu,Renyiming Li,Yan Li,Ruiyu Li,Ruili Li,Jiayi Ding,Eichi Takaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProSAC-CT是一种用于低剂量CT图像去噪的渐进式频谱-解剖协同引导多阶段扩散模型，通过解剖先验引导条件化、残差频域解耦和时间步解耦去噪来提高图像质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT虽然减少了辐射暴露，但引入了更强的量子噪声、条纹伪影和局部纹理退化，这会掩盖解剖边界并削弱低对比度结构。现有的扩散模型方法通常存在解剖引导不足、频率依赖恢复不确定以及统一反向过程建模的问题

Method: 提出ProSAC-CT模型，包含三个核心组件：1) 解剖先验引导条件化(APGC)模块，从LDCT提取结构引导；2) 残差频域解耦阶段(RFDDS)，增强频率感知表示；3) 时间步解耦去噪解码器(TD3)，将不同特征分配给不同的反向扩散阶段，实现解剖稳定、边界细化和细节恢复

Result: 在四个LDCT退化基准测试中，ProSAC-CT在图像保真度、结构相似性、感知质量和信息保存方面优于代表性方法，并能更好地保留边界敏感的解剖细节。在Mayo-2020数据集的下游解剖区域分类任务中进一步表明ProSAC-CT保留了任务相关的解剖信息

Conclusion: ProSAC-CT通过渐进式频谱-解剖协同引导的多阶段扩散框架，有效解决了低剂量CT去噪中的解剖引导不足和频率依赖恢复问题，支持其在低剂量CT去噪中的实际应用

Abstract: Low-dose computed tomography (LDCT) reduces radiation exposure but introduces stronger quantum noise, streak artifacts, and local texture degradation, which can obscure anatomical boundaries and weaken low-contrast structures. Diffusion models are promising for LDCT denoising by progressively recovering normal-dose CT (NDCT) images from degraded LDCT inputs, but existing methods often suffer from insufficient anatomical guidance, uncertain frequency-dependent recovery, and uniform reverse-process modeling. We propose ProSAC-CT, a progressive spectral-anatomical co-guided multi-stage diffusion model for image-domain LDCT denoising. ProSAC-CT integrates an anatomical-prior-guided conditioning (APGC) module, a residual frequency-domain decoupling stage (RFDDS), and a time-step-decoupling denoising decoder (TD3). APGC extracts LDCT-derived structural guidance, RFDDS enhances frequency-aware representations, and TD3 assigns them to different reverse-diffusion stages for anatomical stabilization, boundary refinement, and fine-detail recovery. Experiments on four LDCT degradation benchmarks show that ProSAC-CT improves image fidelity, structural similarity, perceptual quality, and information preservation over representative methods while better preserving boundary-sensitive anatomical details. Downstream anatomical-region classification on Mayo-2020 further indicates that ProSAC-CT retains task-relevant anatomical information, supporting its practical use for low-dose CT denoising.

</details>


### [43] [DL-VINS-Factory: A Modular Framework for Learned Visual Front-Ends in Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01757)
*Shoon Kit Lim,Melissa Jia Ying Chong,Ting Yang Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DL-VINS-Factory是一个统一的视觉-惯性SLAM框架，集成了多种深度学习特征提取器（ALIKED、RaCo、SuperPoint、XFeat），并比较了它们在各种数据集上的性能表现，发现在某些场景下学习型特征优于传统方法，但不是普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习特征在视觉匹配方面表现出色，但其在紧耦合视觉-惯性SLAM（VI-SLAM）中的实际价值尚未得到充分表征。需要系统地评估学习型特征提取器在VI-SLAM系统中的性能表现。

Method: 提出了DL-VINS-Factory统一框架，集成四种学习型特征提取器（ALIKED, RaCo, SuperPoint, XFeat），支持两种匹配方法：Lucas-Kanade光流跟踪或LightGlue描述符匹配。所有前端共享基于滑动窗口的Ceres后端，可选AnyLoc DINOv2-VLAD闭环检测和4-DoF位姿图优化。在四个数据集上进行了基准测试，涵盖室内、非结构化室外、激进运动和视觉退化条件。

Result: 学习型前端在实时嵌入式VI-SLAM中是可行的，但并不普遍优于传统跟踪方法。具体结果：在EuRoC数据集上，ALIKED+LG相对于GFTT+LK基线，单目里程计ATE减少5%，带闭环的立体模式减少7%。在NTU-VIRAL数据集上，ALIKED+LG立体模式带闭环ATE减少12%。在Botanic Garden数据集上，SuperPoint+LK灰度相机ATE减少29%，RaCo+LK RGB相机ATE减少38%。在SubT-MRS数据集上，学习型前端的改进程度因具体情况而异。在Jetson AGX Orin上通过TensorRT加速，所有有效配置都能实时运行（单目29-47 FPS，立体18-33 FPS）。AnyLoc比BRIEF+DBoW2检测到大约2-7倍的有效闭环。

Conclusion: 深度学习特征提取器可以在某些VI-SLAM场景中提供性能改进，特别是在具有挑战性的运动条件或特定传感器配置下，但它们并不是普遍优于传统方法的选择。该框架为研究人员提供了一个统一的测试平台来评估不同的特征提取和匹配策略。

Abstract: Deep-learning features excel in visual matching, yet their practical value in tightly coupled visual-inertial SLAM (VI-SLAM) remains insufficiently characterized. We present DL-VINS-Factory, a unified framework that integrates learned feature extractors (ALIKED, RaCo, SuperPoint, XFeat) with either Lucas--Kanade (LK) optical-flow tracking or LightGlue (LG) descriptor matching. All front-ends share a sliding-window Ceres back-end, with optional AnyLoc DINOv2-VLAD loop closure, and 4-DoF pose-graph optimization. We benchmark the system across the four datasets covering indoor, unstructured outdoor, aggressive-motion, and visually degraded conditions. Results show that learned front-ends are viable for real-time embedded VI-SLAM, but are not universally superior to classical tracking. Relative to the corresponding GFTT+LK baseline, ALIKED+LG reduces EuRoC ATE by $5\%$ in monocular odometry and by $7\%$ in stereo with loop-closure. On NTU-VIRAL, where aggressive aerial motion increases inter-frame viewpoint change, ALIKED+LG stereo reduces loop-closed ATE by $12\%$. In Botanic Garden dataset, optical-flow tracking remains preferable, but learned keypoints still improve over the baseline GFTT, in which SuperPoint+LK reduces grayscale camera ATE by $29\%$, while RaCo+LK reduces RGB camera ATE by $38\%$. On SubT-MRS, learned front-ends display varying degree of improvement based on individual cases. With TensorRT acceleration on a Jetson AGX Orin, all valid configurations run in real time between $29$--$47$ FPS in monocular mode and $18$--$33$ FPS in stereo mode for the EuRoC and NTU-VIRAL datasets. AnyLoc further confirms roughly $2$--$7\times$ more valid loops than BRIEF+DBoW2. The implementation is open-sourced at https://github.com/limshoonkit/DL-VINS-Factory-ROS2/.

</details>


### [44] [ProCal: Inference-Time Proposal Calibration for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01759)
*Jae-Ryung Hong,Ho-Joong Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProCal（提案校准）方法，通过结合定位感知的前景分数和背景感知的抑制分数来改进开放词汇目标检测中新颖类别的定位质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测旨在检测训练时未见过的新颖类别。现有方法利用冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）作为检测器主干，但VLM的分类分数缺乏对图像中物体位置和尺度的识别能力。作者观察到预训练的VLM能够区分前景和背景区域，因此需要改进新颖类别检测中的定位质量。

Method: 提出推理时的提案校准（ProCal）方法：1）定位感知前景分数：捕捉提案是否包含物体区域；2）背景感知抑制分数：衡量提案与背景的相似程度；3）通过结合这两种分数计算提案先验，改进分类分数的定位质量，抑制背景提案上的错误新颖激活。

Result: 在CLIPSelf ViT-L/14上应用ProCal，在OV-LVIS数据集上将APr提升了+2.5。分析表明，提案级的定位感知重新排序能够缓解新颖物体的排名误校准问题，并且ProCal能一致地将真正的新颖提案排在背景和部分新颖提案之上。

Conclusion: ProCal通过结合定位感知和背景感知机制，有效改进了开放词汇目标检测中新颖类别的定位质量，证明了在推理时进行提案级校准的简单而有效的方法，能够缓解VLM分类分数缺乏位置和尺度信息的问题。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to localize and classify objects beyond the fixed set of categories seen dur ing training. Recent open-vocabulary object detection methods improve localization and classification for unseen categories by leveraging a frozen VLM as a detector backbone. However, VLM classification score lacks recognizing position and scale of the object in an image. We observe that pretrained VLMs en able to classify foreground and background regions. According to this observation, we propose a simple inference-time Pro posal Calibration (ProCal) that improves localization quality of the classification score. ProCal computes a proposal prior by combining two scores: localization-aware foreground score and background-aware suppression score. Localization-aware foreground score captures whether a proposal contains an object area. Background-aware suppression score measures the extent to which the proposal resembles background. We analyze that ProCal suppresses false novel activation on background proposals and consistently ranks true novel proposals above background and partial novel proposals. Applied to CLIPSelf ViT-L/14, ProCal improves APr +2.5 on OV-LVIS. The analyses show that proposal-level localization-aware reranking effects to mitigate ranking miscalibration for novel objects.

</details>


### [45] [JointHOI: Jointly Generating Contact Maps Enhances Hand Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01768)
*Mingyeong Song,Jungbin Cho,Jisoo Kim,Ananya Bal,Kartik Sharma,Youngjae Yu,Laszlo A. Jeni,Junhyug Noh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JointHOI是一个单阶段扩散框架，从文本联合生成3D手物交互运动和动态距离接触图，通过将接触作为辅助内部模态，在训练中学习接触-运动耦合，在推理时通过接触引导采样改善物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的手物交互生成在沉浸式应用和机器人学中受到关注，但生成物理上合理的交互仍具挑战。现有方法存在接触错误导致的手物漂浮和穿透等明显伪影，且通常依赖多阶段流程，未能建模时间演化的接触关系。

Method: 提出JointHOI单阶段扩散框架：1) 将接触作为辅助内部模态与手物运动联合生成；2) 学习接触-运动耦合关系；3) 在推理时采用接触引导采样，确保生成的接触图与运动隐含几何一致性；4) 使用基于距离的动态接触图表示。

Result: 在GRAB和ARCTIC数据集上的实验表明，相比先前方法，JointHOI在文本遵循和物理合理性方面取得一致改进，减少了穿透和漂浮问题，提高了时间稳定性。

Conclusion: JointHOI通过将接触作为内部模态进行联合生成，能够学习接触与运动间的耦合关系，接触引导采样确保了物理一致性，为文本驱动的手物交互生成提供了更高质量且物理合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Text driven hand object interaction (HOI) generation is gaining attention for immersive applications and robotics, yet producing physically plausible interactions remains challenging. Even when individual motions appear natural, small contact errors can cause conspicuous artifacts such as floating and interpenetration. Prior methods mitigate these issues using explicit contact cues or implicit grasp priors, but typically rely on multi stage pipelines and fail to model temporally evolving contact. We present JointHOI, a single stage diffusion framework that jointly generates 3D hand object motion and dynamic, distance based contact maps from text. By treating contact as an auxiliary inner modality, joint generation enables the model to learn contact motion coupling during training. At inference, contact guided sampling enforces consistency between generated contact maps and motion implied geometry, improving temporal stability and reducing penetration and floating. Experiments on GRAB and ARCTIC demonstrate consistent improvements in text adherence and physical plausibility over prior methods.

</details>


### [46] [LLM-Empowered Multimodal Fusion Framework for Autonomous Driving: Semantic Enhancement and Channel-Adaptive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01772)
*Wen Wang,Yaping Sun,Yejun He,Hao Chen,Zhiyong Chen,Xiaodong Xu,Nan Ma,Shuguang Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LM-SCIP框架利用大语言模型作为核心推理器，动态融合视觉和雷达信息，通过信道自适应机制处理雷达输入质量变化，在遮挡和恶劣天气条件下实现鲁棒的自动驾驶感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-雷达融合方法在面对动态变化的输入质量时（如遮挡、恶劣天气、信道噪声）存在根本性挑战，这些因素会影响融合质量。需要从静态数据融合转向信道感知的语义推理方法。

Method: 提出LM-SCIP框架：1) 采用分层雷达-视觉编码器；2) 引入信道自适应语义模块(CASM)，将链路指标映射为"信道提示"来动态门控外部雷达特征；3) 使用参数高效的LoRA调优LLM结合异构专家混合(H-MoE)仲裁本地视觉线索和信道条件雷达上下文；4) 通过解耦的多任务解码器输出定位、轨迹预测和图像重建。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，可控切换雷达输入时，LM-SCIP将定位RMSE降低了40.0%；在VIRAT数据集上，模型达到0.214m定位RMSE和0.179m minFDE(k=1)。结果表明，在低信噪比时能实现鲁棒的视觉主导回退，在高信噪比时能实现协同融合。

Conclusion: LM-SCIP框架成功将视觉-雷达融合问题从静态数据融合重新定义为信道感知的语义推理，通过LLM为核心的架构实现了对动态变化输入质量的鲁棒处理，显著提升了自动驾驶感知系统的性能。

Abstract: Vision-radar fusion is central to robust autonomous driving, combining dense visual semantics with precise range and velocity measurements from radar. However, real-world fusion quality is fundamentally challenged by dynamically varying input quality, stemming from occlusion, adverse weather, and channel noise. To address this, we re-frame the problem from static data fusion to channel-aware semantic reasoning and propose a Large Language Model-centric Semantic-layer Channel-aware Integrated Perception (LM-SCIP) framework. It places a Large Language Model (LLM) as a central reasoning core to fuse a local visual stream with a quality-varying external radar stream used to cover perception-blind spots. Concretely, LM-SCIP couples a hierarchical radar-vision encoder with a Channel-Adaptive Semantic Module (CASM) that maps link indicators into a "Channel Prompt" to dynamically gate external radar features. A parameter-efficient, LoRA-tuned LLM, in conjunction with a heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts (H-MoE), then arbitrates between local visual cues and the channel-conditioned radar context. Finally, a decoupled multi-task decoder outputs localization, trajectory forecasting, and image reconstruction. Experiments on nuScenes and VIRAT validate our approach. On nuScenes, under a controlled toggle of radar input, LM-SCIP reduces localization RMSE by 40.0% versus a vision-only baseline. On VIRAT, the model attains a 0.214m localization RMSE and 0.179m minFDE (k=1). These results reveal that the proposed LM-SCIP enables a robust vision-dominant fallback at low SNR and synergistic fusion at high SNR.

</details>


### [47] [SpaceEra++: A Unified Framework Towards 3D Spatial Reasoning in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01784)
*Weili Guan,Haoyu Zhang,Meng Liu,Qianlong Xiang,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceEra++是一个扩展的视觉空间理解框架，通过ScenePick帧采样策略和SpaceAlign空间对齐模块，解决了原始SpaceEra框架因扫描视频输入不足和弱推理约束而受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉语言模型在空间理解方面存在两个主要限制：1) 由于基于2D观察而产生的空间不确定性；2) 用于3D空间理解的数据稀缺。虽然原始SpaceEra框架在NeurIPS 2025上取得了显著性能提升，但作者发现其有效性仍受扫描视频输入不足和弱推理约束的阻碍。

Method: SpaceEra++是一个综合系统，涵盖数据构造、模型设计、训练优化和提示推理。主要包含两个关键技术：1) ScenePick：一个平衡空间覆盖度和对象语义的帧采样策略，用于产生紧凑而全面的场景表示；2) SpaceAlign：通过联合利用绝对坐标和相对空间关系来增强对对象约束，使优化与空间准确性对齐。

Result: 在多个基准测试上进行的大量实验显示，该系统相较于强基线模型取得了持续改进。消融研究验证了每个组件的个体和联合贡献，进一步的分析为未来研究提供了指导。

Conclusion: SpaceEra++通过解决输入不足和推理约束问题，显著提升了视觉空间理解能力。该系统在数据采样和空间对齐方面的创新方法为视觉语言模型的空间理解任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual-spatial understanding, defined as the ability to infer object relationships and scene layouts from visual inputs, is fundamental to downstream tasks such as robotic navigation and embodied interaction. However, pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) remain constrained by spatial uncertainty stemming from inherently 2D observations and by the scarcity of data for 3D spatial understanding. To address these limitations, we proposed a novel framework, SpaceEra, in the NeurIPS 2025 Spotlight paper. Although it achieved significant performance gains, we further observed that its effectiveness is hindered by insufficient input from scanning videos and weak reasoning constraints. To tackle these newly emerged challenges, we extend the original framework into a comprehensive system, termed SpaceEra++, which spans data construction, model design, training optimization, and prompting inference. Specifically, to alleviate input insufficiency, we introduce ScenePick, a frame sampling strategy that balances spatial coverage with object semantics to produce compact yet comprehensive scene representations. In addition, to enhance spatial reasoning, we develop SpaceAlign, which enforces pairwise object constraints by jointly exploiting absolute coordinates and relative spatial relations, thereby aligning optimization with spatial accuracy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, while ablation studies validate both the individual and joint contributions of each component, and further analyses provide guidance for future research.

</details>


### [48] [PixGS: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Direct 3D Gaussian Splat Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01803)
*Duy Cao,Phong Nguyen-Ha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixGS是一个单阶段、直接生成高质量3D高斯溅射(3DGS)的方法，通过像素空间扩散绕过了有损潜在压缩，在单个A100 GPU上实现了1秒的快速推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本或图像生成3D内容的方法存在视图不一致和高质量3D数据稀缺的问题。当前解决方案通常依赖于训练复杂的级联管道，计算成本高且可扩展性有限。更重要的是，生成的3D资产质量受到组件容量和压缩潜在空间的固有约束，导致解码伪影和累积错误。

Method: 提出了一个单阶段管道PixGS，直接进行高质量3DGS生成。该方法利用像素空间扩散的最新进展来绕过有损潜在压缩，同时仍然受益于大量的2D生成先验。通过直接在每个时间步去噪3D高斯属性，实现了外观和几何的精确、溅射级别的正则化。引入了一个全面的监督策略，包含了表面法线、深度和高频结构信息。

Result: 实验表明PixGS超越了当前最先进的方法，同时保持了快速的推理速度（在单个A100 GPU上为1秒）。该方法为多阶段生成管道提供了一个鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: PixGS通过单阶段、直接生成高质量3D高斯溅射的方法，解决了现有方法中的计算效率和质量限制问题。该方法通过利用像素空间扩散和全面监督策略，实现了卓越的性能和效率，为3D内容生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D content generation from text or images have achieved impressive results, yet view inconsistency from 2D generators and the scarcity of high-quality 3D data remain significant bottlenecks. Existing solutions typically adapt large-scale pre-trained text-to-image latent diffusion models to generate 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS). However, these approaches often rely on training complex cascade pipelines that are computationally expensive and scalability-limited. Most critically, the quality of generated 3D assets is inherently constrained by each component capacity and compressed latent space, leading to decoding artifacts and accumulated errors. To address these limitations, we propose PixGS, a single-stage pipeline for direct high-quality 3DGS generation, which leverages recent advances in pixel-space diffusion to bypass lossy latent compression while still benefiting from the vast 2D generative priors. By directly denoising 3D Gaussian attributes at each timestep, our method enables precise, splat-level regularization of both appearance and geometry. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive supervision strategy that incorporates surface normals, depth, and high-frequency structural information, which is often overlooked in prior works. Experiments demonstrate that PixGS outperforms current state-of-the-art methods while maintaining a fast inference speed (1s on a single A100 GPU), offering a robust and efficient alternative to multi-stage generation pipelines.

</details>


### [49] [MMBench-Live: A Continuously Evolving Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01813)
*Yuanzhi Liu,Shousheng Zhao,Bo Zhou,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMBench-Live：一个通过多智能体驱动自动化管道构建的持续演进的多模态基准测试，解决了静态基准测试的时间过时、数据污染和成本高昂的维护问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数多模态基准测试是静态的，容易受到时间过时性、数据污染和昂贵维护成本的影响。需要一种可持续的、可扩展的多模态基准测试演化范式。

Method: 1. 将基准测试演化视为任务引导的数据集构建
2. 结构化基准测试规范
3. 反馈控制的实时数据采集
4. 可验证的问答生成与可执行推理
5. 分布一致更新策略：从原始基准测试中提取任务相关的视觉模式来指导数据收集和过滤

Result: 1. 从MMBench实例化，包含5.9K个新生成的评估实例，答案正确率高
2. 每次更新成本约30美元，耗时1-2小时
3. 保持了稳定的模型排名
4. 保持与原始基准测试的语义对齐
5. 表现出较弱的数据污染相关记忆信号

Conclusion: MMBench-Live为可持续的多模态基准测试演化提供了一个实用且可扩展的范式，通过自动化管道实现低成本、高效率的基准测试更新，同时保持评估的稳定性和可比性。

Abstract: Evaluation benchmarks are essential for assessing vision-language models (VLMs), but most multimodal benchmarks are static, making them vulnerable to temporal staleness, data contamination, and costly maintenance. We present MMBench-Live, a continuously evolving multimodal benchmark built by a multi-agent-driven automated pipeline. Our framework treats benchmark evolution as task-guided dataset construction, integrating structured benchmark specification, feedback-controlled real-time data acquisition, and verifiable QA generation with executable reasoning. To maintain cross-version comparability, we introduce a distribution-consistent update strategy that extracts task-related visual patterns from the original benchmark to guide data collection and filtering. Instantiated from MMBench, MMBench-Live contains 5.9K newly generated evaluation instances with a high answer correctness rate, while each update costs about USD 30 and takes 1-2 hours. Extensive evaluations show that MMBench-Live preserves stable model rankings, maintains semantic alignment with the original benchmark, and exhibits weaker contamination-related memorization signals, suggesting a practical and scalable paradigm for sustainable multimodal benchmark evolution. The project is available at https://github.com/PRIS-CV/MMBench-Live.

</details>


### [50] [Rethinking Conditional Generation for Underwater Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01825)
*Hua Li,Yongjie Weng,Yutong Li,Zhiyuan Li,Runmin Cong,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCGNet（Degradation-aware Conditional Generation Network），一种专门针对水下环境的显著性目标检测方法，通过动态多粒度模块、水下物理先验模块和空间高斯模块来解决水下图像的低对比度、光照不均和颜色失真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下显著性目标检测面临独特挑战：低对比度、不均匀光照、颜色失真（由散射和吸收效应引起），传统SOD方法在水下环境中效果有限，需要专门针对水下退化特征的方法。

Method: 1. 动态多粒度模块（DMG）：基于人类视觉系统，鲁棒检测不同尺度和模糊边界的显著物体；
2. 水下物理先验模块（UPP）：利用伪深度引导估计水下光衰减和后向散射，恢复退化感知的RGB特征；
3. 水下空间高斯模块（USG）：从最强引导响应构建空间高斯显著性先验，增强物体中心区域；
4. 轻量级时间步自适应扩散Transformer（DiT）瓶颈：插入去噪解码器，在不同扩散时间步精炼融合特征。

Result: 在USOD10K、USOD、CSOD10K、MAS3K和RMAS五个数据集上的综合实验表明，DCGNet显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在复杂水下视觉应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: DCGNet通过结合人类视觉感知、水下物理建模和空间高斯先验，有效解决了水下显著性检测的关键挑战，为水下视觉任务提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Salient Object Detection in underwater images remains challenging due to low contrast, uneven illumination, and color distortion caused by scattering and absorption effects, which limit the effectiveness of conventional SOD methods in underwater environments. To address these challenges, we propose a Degradation-aware Conditional Generation Network (DCGNet), specifically designed to construct reliable conditional features for underwater saliency generation. First, we design a Dynamic Multi-Granularity module (DMG) grounded in the human visual system to robustly detect salient objects of varying scales with blurred boundaries. Then, we develop an Underwater Physics-Prior module (UPP), which utilizes pseudo-depth guidance to estimate underwater light attenuation and backscatter, thereby restoring degradation-aware RGB features and mitigating color distortion and boundary ambiguity. Based on the physics-guided representation, we introduce an Underwater Spatial Gaussian module (USG), which constructs a spatial Gaussian saliency prior from the strongest guided response to enhance object-centered salient regions and suppress cluttered underwater backgrounds. In addition, a lightweight timestep-adaptive Diffusion Transformer (DiT) bottleneck is inserted into the denoising decoder to refine fused features at different diffusion timesteps. Comprehensive experiments on USOD10K, USOD, CSOD10K, MAS3K, and RMAS demonstrate that DCGNet significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, verifying its potential for complex underwater visual applications.

</details>


### [51] [C2E: Boosting Ego-Only 3D Object Detection via Multi-Teacher Contrastive Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01827)
*Jinlong Wang,Xun Huang,Qiming Xia,Shijia Zhao,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M2S框架，通过协作感知到自我感知的知识蒸馏，在不增加通信成本的情况下提升3D目标检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自我感知系统在复杂室外环境中存在视角受限和遮挡问题，而多智能体协作感知虽然性能优异但面临高通信成本和姿态误差累积等实际应用障碍

Method: 提出C2E范式下的M2S对比知识蒸馏框架，包含多级特征增强模块、辅助点云重建和多教师对比蒸馏机制，以缓解C2E范式中的域差距问题

Result: 在V2XSet、V2V4Real和DAIR-V2X数据集上的实验表明，结合CoSDH等先进3D检测器，M2S框架能实现最高8.64%的3D mAP性能提升，且不引入额外通信成本

Conclusion: M2S框架能够在保持协作感知优异性能的同时，有效避免通信延迟和定位误差等问题，为自动驾驶3D目标检测提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is essential for autonomous driving systems. However, traditional Ego-only Perception (Eo-Perception) suffers from limited perspective and occlusions in a complex outdoor environment, leading to performance bottlenecks. Recently, research on multi-agent Collaborative Perception (Co-Perception) has demonstrated excellent performance, but high communication costs and accumulated pose error hinder its application. To address this, we explore a novel C2E (Co-Perception to Eo-Perception) paradigm through the Multi-to-Single (M2S) agent contrastive knowledge distillation framework. Our M2S framework first designs Multi-Level Feature Enhancement module to provide more stable features, and introduces Auxiliary Point Cloud Reconstruction and Multi-Teacher Contrastive Distillation mechanisms to mitigate domain gaps in point cloud and feature distributions within the C2E paradigm. Benefiting from this, our M2S can retain the excellent performance of collaborative perception while effectively avoiding the drawbacks, such as communication delays and positioning errors. Extensive experiments on the V2XSet, V2V4Real and DAIR-V2X datasets show the effectiveness and generalizability of our M2S framework when combined with the state-of-the-art CoSDH model and other excellent 3D detectors. Our M2S framework can deliver up to a 8.64% improvement in 3D mAP performance without introducing any communication costs.

</details>


### [52] [Geometric Foundation Model Distillation for Efficient Lunar 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01851)
*Clémentine Grethen,Florient Chouteau,Géraldine Morin,Simone Gasparini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何通过知识蒸馏将大型3D基础模型MASt3R压缩为轻量级模型，用于月球立体重建任务。提出了结构化SVD初始化方法来桥接师生模型维度差异，实现了7倍模型压缩同时保持重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型3D基础模型（如688M参数的MASt3R）在立体重建方面达到最先进水平，但计算需求高，难以部署在硬件受限的环境中（如行星探索领域）。需要研究如何通过知识蒸馏有效压缩此类模型，以适应严格的计算资源限制。

Method: 1. 使用经过月球影像微调的MASt3R教师模型；2. 将密集几何预测蒸馏到不同编码器类型（CNN vs ViT）、不同解码器宽度和深度的轻量级学生模型家族；3. 提出结构化SVD初始化方法，将教师解码器权重投影到学生模型的潜在空间，提供良好的初始起点；4. 比较特征级蒸馏与输出级监督的效果。

Result: 1. 获得的学生模型在模型大小减少最多7倍的情况下，保持了教师模型大部分重建精度；2. 蒸馏学生模型甚至优于直接使用稀疏地面真值注释训练的基线；3. SVD初始化显著改善了收敛性和最终性能；4. 发现在几何基础模型蒸馏中：Transformer编码器优于卷积编码器，保持编码器容量比维持大解码器更重要，特征级蒸馏始终优于仅输出监督。

Conclusion: 研究提供了在资源受限环境中部署3D重建模型的实用指南。通过结构化SVD初始化和知识蒸馏，可以有效压缩大型几何基础模型，同时保持性能。这些发现为行星探索等计算受限领域的3D重建模型部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large 3D foundation models such as MASt3R achieve state-of-the-art stereo reconstruction but are computationally demanding for deployment under strict hardware constraints -- a critical limitation in domains such as planetary exploration, where onboard computing is severely restricted. We study how far such models can be compressed through knowledge distillation, using lunar stereo reconstruction as a challenging and practically relevant case study. Starting from a 688M-parameter MASt3R teacher fine-tuned on lunar imagery, we distill its dense geometric predictions into a family of lightweight students spanning different encoder types (CNN vs ViT), decoder widths and depths, and training strategies. To bridge the dimensional mismatch between teacher and student, we propose a structured SVD-based initialization that projects the teacher's decoder weights into the student's smaller latent space, yielding a warm start that significantly improves convergence and final performance. Based on our results on lunar data, we can obtain a distilled student that retains most of teacher's reconstruction accuracy while reducing the model size up to 7 times, and even outperforms a baseline trained directly with sparse ground-truth annotations. Beyond compression, our study highlights both principles and practical insights for distilling geometric foundation models: a convolutional encoder underperforms transformer-based alternatives (though pretraining availability remains a confounding factor), preserving encoder capacity is more critical than maintaining a large decoder, feature-level distillation consistently outperforms output-only supervision, and SVD-based initialization improves optimisation stability. These findings provide practical guidelines for deploying 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [53] [QWERTY: Training-Free Motion Control via Query-Warped Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01869)
*Kyobin Choo,Youngmin Kim,Hyunkyung Han,Geunrip Park,Chanyoung Kim,Sunyoung Jung,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QWERTY是一种无需训练的方法，通过在预训练的图像到视频扩散变压器中操纵3D全注意力机制，使用用户定义的对象形变和光流实现灵活的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散变压器(DiTs)虽然能生成高质量的时间一致性视频，但运动控制主要依赖文本提示，缺乏显式控制。现有方法需要大量微调和计算成本，可能会损害预训练模型的生成能力。

Method: 通过形变查询的帧不变语义子空间来操纵DiTs的3D全注意力机制。使用查询形变后的DiT预测的噪声作为扩散轨迹引导，并利用该噪声作为潜在优化的自引导来提升控制稳定性和视觉质量。

Result: 实验表明，QWERTY在现有的无训练方法中实现了最有效的运动控制，在最近的图像到视频DiT上性能与基于微调的方法相当。

Conclusion: QWERTY为预训练的视频扩散变压器提供了一种无需训练的运动控制框架，通过注意力操纵实现了有效的运动指导，避免了微调的高昂成本。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) generate high-fidelity and temporally coherent videos, yet motion control remains implicit, primarily relying on text prompts. As a result, achieving desired motion often requires extensive prompt engineering and repeated resampling. While fine-tuning models with additional spatial prompts (e.g., bounding boxes or point trajectories) enables explicit control, it demands substantial data curation and computation, and may compromise the generative capabilities of pretrained models. Consequently, training-free motion control using such spatial prompts has been explored in U-Net-based video diffusion models, but remains largely unexplored for DiTs. We introduce QWERTY, a training-free framework that enables flexible motion control in pretrained image-to-video DiTs via user-defined object warping and optical flow. We carefully manipulate the 3D full attention of DiTs by warping the frame-invariant semantic subspace of queries. We find that the noise predicted by the query-warped DiT naturally guides the diffusion trajectory toward the desired motion, and further show that leveraging this noise as self-guidance for latent optimization improves control stability and visual quality. Experiments show that QWERTY achieves the most effective motion control among existing training-free approaches on a recent image-to-video DiT, with performance comparable to fine-tuning-based methods.

</details>


### [54] [Descriptor: LYNRED Mobility Dataset Multimodal Detection Subset (LYNRED-MDS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01871)
*Loïc Arbez,Jessy Matias,Xavier Brenière,Jocelyn Chanussot,Ronald Phlypo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LYNRED-MDS数据集：一个包含4000对RGB-红外图像的多模态数据集，覆盖法国格勒诺布尔地区多种天气、光照和道路条件，用于提升低能见度条件下的行人检测和碰撞预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路安全系统主要关注碰撞后损害最小化，而算法感知的进步使焦点转向早期碰撞预测。在夜间或雾天等低能见度条件下，热红外传感优于人眼视觉和RGB成像。现有RGB-红外数据集（如FLIR ADAS和LLVIP）主要包含清晰天气和简单场景，缺乏多样性。

Method: 作者引入了LYNRED-MDS：多模态检测子集，这是LYNRED移动数据集的一个子集。该数据集包含4000对RGB-红外图像对，在法国格勒诺布尔地区多种天气、光照和道路条件下采集。涵盖城市、乡村、山区等不同驾驶场景，使用符合西欧标准的车队。

Result: 使用YOLOv8n基线模型进行热红外跨数据集评估表明，该数据集在驾驶场景行人检测方面具有良好的泛化潜力。通过覆盖关键边缘情况，该数据集支持开发更可靠和可部署的高级驾驶辅助系统视觉系统。

Conclusion: LYNRED-MDS数据集通过提供多样化的多模态数据（特别是涵盖低能见度条件下的热红外图像），显著提升了行人检测系统在实际复杂驾驶环境中的泛化能力和可靠性，有助于推动先进驾驶辅助系统向早期碰撞预测方向发展。

Abstract: Current road safety systems primarily focus on minimizing post-collision damage. However, advances in algorithmic perception are shifting focus toward early collision prediction, especially in lowvisibility conditions like nighttime or fog, where thermal infrared sensing outperforms both human vision and RGB imaging. While available RGB-infrared datasets such as FLIR ADAS and LLVIP are good benchmarks, they mostly consist of clear weather and overly simple scenarios. In this article, we introduce the LYNRED-MDS: Multimodal Detection Subset, a subset of the LYNRED Mobility Dataset, comprised of 4000 RGB-infrared image pairs captured under diverse weather, lighting, and road conditions around Grenoble, France. Our dataset spans varied driving contexts (urban, rural, mountainous, etc.) and a vehicle fleet compliant with Western European standards. Thermal cross-dataset evaluation using a YOLOv8n baseline suggests that our dataset offers strong generalization potential for pedestrian detection in driving scenarios. By covering critical edge cases, our dataset supports the development of more reliable and deployable vision systems for advanced driver-assistance systems.

</details>


### [55] [SAB-LVLM: Significance-Aware Binarization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01876)
*Qi Lyu,Jiahua Dong,Baichen Liu,Xudong Wang,Mingfei Han,Yulun Zhang,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAB-LVLM是一种针对大型视觉语言模型的显著性感知二值化方法，通过Hessian矩阵构建和空间显著性映射来识别模态特异性权重，实现约1位压缩的同时最小化性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在资源受限设备上部署面临存储和延迟挑战。现有二值化方法忽视不同层和模态间权重的重要性差异，导致任务无关参数被保留而模态关键权重未充分优化，造成显著性能下降。

Method: 首先为文本和视觉输入构建Hessian矩阵，提出空间显著性映射区分单模态激活和多模态激活的全精度权重。然后设计模态引导集成策略获得显著性感知二值化图，量化各层各模态的权重重要性。将该图作为误差重加权项融入二值化目标，通过交替显著性加权更新方案执行二值化拟合。

Result: 在约1位压缩约束下，SAB-LVLM优于现有二进制PTQ方法，显著降低了模型存储和计算成本的同时保持了较好的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的显著性感知二值化方法有效解决了大型视觉语言模型量化中模态权重重要性差异的问题，实现了高效压缩而不显著牺牲性能，为资源受限设备上的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in multimodal understanding, yet their enormous parameter scale and cross-modal computation incur substantial memory and latency overhead, severely limiting real-world deployment on resource-constrained devices. Binarization offers an attractive solution by drastically reducing storage and computational costs. However, existing binarization methods neglect the varying importance of weights across different layers and modalities. This causes parameters irrelevant to downstream tasks to be unnecessarily retained, whereas modality-critical weights may not be adequately optimized, resulting in significant performance degradation. To address these challenges, we develop a novel \underline{S}ignificance-\underline{A}ware \underline{B}inarization for \underline{L}arge \underline{V}ision-\underline{L}anguage \underline{M}odels (SAB-LVLM). Specifically, after constructing Hessian matrices for textual and visual inputs, we propose a spatial significance map to distinguish full-precision weights activated under a single modality from those activated across modalities. We then devise a modality-guided integration strategy to obtain the significance-aware binarization map, which measures weight significance across layers and modalities. Subsequently, this binarization map is incorporated into the binarization objective as an error reweighting term, and binarization fitting is performed through an alternating significance-weighted update scheme. Extensive experiments illustrate the superiority of our SAB-LVLM over existing binary PTQ methods under an approximately 1-bit compression constraint. Our code is accessible at https://github.com/LyuQi127/SAB_LVLM.

</details>


### [56] [Diversity-aware View Partitioning for Scalable VGGT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01885)
*Jinsoo Park,Donggyu Choi,Ahyun Seo,Minsu cho,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个无需训练、即插即用的VGGT推理框架，通过基于视觉差异和空间分散的组合图划分将视图组织成多样性感知的平衡块，提高多视图重建性能同时减少内存使用和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何变换器如VGGT在处理大规模视图集合时面临注意力机制二次成本的问题，且重建质量对视点分布敏感。简单地增加视图数量而缺乏足够的视点多样性反而会降低性能，因为冗余视图会引入高度相似的token，稀释注意力机制中的信息几何信号。

Method: 提出训练免费、即插即用的VGGT推理框架：1) 通过组合图划分将视图组织成多样性感知的平衡块；2) 基于视觉差异和空间分散进行划分；3) 使用软姿态传播策略近似空间关系，仅需少量种子帧；4) 减少冗余注意力交互，聚焦于几何信息丰富的视图。

Result: 大量实验表明，在相机姿态估计、多视图深度预测和3D重建任务中性能得到提升，同时减少了内存使用和推理延迟。该框架也能补充现有的VGGT变体，在不牺牲几何保真度的情况下实现可扩展的多视图重建。

Conclusion: 通过组织视图为多样性感知的平衡块，可以有效解决VGGT在大规模视图集合中的可扩展性问题，减少冗余注意力交互，提高重建质量，同时保持推理效率，为多视图几何重建提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Geometry transformers such as VGGT achieve strong performance by jointly reasoning over multiple views with global attention. However, scaling them to large view collections remains challenging due to the quadratic cost of attention. Moreover, our empirical analysis reveals that the reconstruction quality in VGGT is sensitive to the distribution of viewpoints. Simply increasing the number of views without sufficient viewpoint diversity can even degrade performance, as redundant views introduce highly similar tokens that dilute informative geometric signals in the attention mechanism. Motivated by this observation, we propose a training-free and plug-and-play VGGT inference framework that organizes views into diversity-aware balanced chunks. The chunks are constructed through combinatorial graph partitioning over visual dissimilarity and spatial dispersion. This view organization allows the transformer to focus attention on geometrically informative views while reducing redundant attention interactions. To estimate spatial dispersion without full pose estimation, we approximate spatial relationships via a soft pose propagation strategy based on visual similarity from a small set of seed frames. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved performance in camera pose estimation, multi-view depth prediction, and 3D reconstruction while reducing memory usage and inference latency. Our framework also complements existing VGGT variants, enabling scalable multi-view reconstruction without sacrificing geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [57] [FoundDP: Revisiting Weak Disparity Observability in Dual-Pixel Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01900)
*Fengchen He,Hao Xu,Dayang Zhao,Tingwei Quan,Shaoqun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoundDP是一个结合了双像素相机度规深度估计和单目深度基础模型全局结构先验的统一框架，通过ViT特征对齐缓解了双像素散焦模糊导致的特征退化，在弱视差区域提升了结构一致性和度规精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双像素(DP)成像虽能通过子孔径视差实现单相机的度规深度估计，但极小的有效基线限制了视差可观测性，导致在无纹理、低对比度或下采样区域出现结构退化和深度失效。现有DP方法主要依赖局部视差线索，在视差信号弱或模糊时不可靠。

Method: 提出FoundDP统一框架，将DP度规深度与单目深度基础模型的全局结构先验结合。通过DP衍生深度保持度规尺度，利用ViT特征恢复弱视差区域的结构一致性。采用ViT特征对齐缓解DP散焦模糊导致的ViT表示退化，实现稳定的度规引导深度估计。

Result: 在合成和真实世界DP基准测试上的广泛实验表明，FoundDP在结构保真度和度规准确性方面表现优异，尤其在视差可观测性降低的情况下保持了稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: FoundDP成功解决了DP成像中因基线极小导致的视差可观测性限制问题，通过结合全局结构先验和度规深度，在弱视差区域实现了显著改进，为单相机度规深度估计提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Dual-pixel (DP) imaging enables metric depth estimation from a single camera using sub-aperture disparity. However, the extremely small effective baseline limits disparity observability, leading to structural degradation and depth failure in textureless, low-contrast, or downsampled regions. Existing DP-based methods rely primarily on local disparity cues and therefore become unreliable when disparity signals are weak or ambiguous. To address this limitation, we propose \emph{FoundDP}, a unified framework that integrates metric DP depth with global structural priors from a monocular depth foundation model. Our method preserves metric scale through DP-derived depth and leverages Vision Transformer (ViT) features to restore structural consistency in weak-disparity regions. To ensure reliable metric guidance under DP imaging conditions, we identify and mitigate ViT representation degradation induced by DP defocus blur via ViT feature alignment, enabling stable metric-guided depth estimation. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world DP benchmarks show that FoundDP delivers superior performance, with consistent gains in structural fidelity and metric accuracy, especially under reduced disparity observability. Code will be available at: https://github.com/EchoLighting/FoundDP

</details>


### [58] [Rethinking Post-Hoc Calibration in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01902)
*Tristan Kirscher,Kim-Celine Kahl,Balint Kovacs,Maximilian R. Rokuss,Klaus Maier-Hein,Xavier Coubez,Philippe Meyer,Sylvain Faisan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对语义分割中的后校准方法，解决了两个关键问题：平移不变性缺失和决策保持，提出了平移不变校准器和决策保持校准器，在自然图像和医学分割基准测试中改善了校准性能并防止分割退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型通常存在校准不良问题，在安全关键场景中过度自信的错误可能导致严重后果。虽然后校准方法提供了一种无需重新训练模型的校正置信度的方法，但在密集预测任务中存在两个常被忽视的结构性问题：校准器对对数表示的任意偏移依赖导致相同预测分布可能产生不同校准概率，以及基于似然目标拟合的校准与分割模型训练使用的任务特定指标不匹配。

Method: 首先定义了平移不变校准器，分析了常见校准器是否满足该性质，并为对平移敏感的校准器构建了平移不变对应版本。其次研究了在argmax保持和顺序保持约束下的决策保持校准。由于这些约束会将仿射softmax校准器坍缩为温度缩放，引入了类别条件仿射校准器，这些校准器可以在保持更大表达性的同时实现决策保持，从而量化由决策保持引起的校准-分割权衡。

Result: 在自然图像和医学分割基准测试以及基于扰动的协变量偏移下，匹配比较表明：平移不变变体通常能改善校准指标，而决策保持变体能防止分割退化并保持强大的校准性能。这些结果为语义分割中定义良好的后校准流程提供了实用设计原则。

Conclusion: 研究解决了语义分割后校准中的两个关键结构性问题，提出的平移不变和决策保持校准器在多个数据集上表现出优越性能，为构建稳健的后校准流程提供了理论框架和实用指导。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are essential in semantic segmentation, especially in safety-critical settings where overconfident errors can mislead downstream decisions. Yet modern segmentation models often remain miscalibrated. Post-hoc calibration offers a practical way to correct confidence estimates without retraining the segmentation model, but its use in dense prediction raises structural issues that are often overlooked. We study two such issues. First, adding a constant to all logits leaves the softmax probabilities unchanged, but several standard calibrators can still depend on this arbitrary offset. As a result, two logit representations encoding the same predictive distribution may yield different calibrated probabilities. We define translation-invariant (TI) calibrators as those whose outputs are unchanged under such shifts, characterize which common calibrators satisfy this property, and construct TI counterparts of shift-sensitive calibrators to isolate the effect of removing representation dependence. Second, post-hoc calibration is typically fitted by minimizing a likelihood-based objective, whereas segmentation models are trained with task-specific metrics such as Dice. This mismatch can cause calibration to alter class orderings and degrade the deployed segmentation map. We study decision-preserving calibration under argmax- and order-preservation constraints. Since enforcing these constraints collapses affine softmax calibrators to temperature scaling, we introduce class-conditional affine calibrators that can be made argmax- or order-preserving while retaining greater expressivity, allowing us to quantify the calibration-segmentation trade-off induced by decision preservation. Across natural-image and medical segmentation benchmarks, and under corruption-based covariate shift, matched comparisons show that TI variants generally improve calibration metrics, while decision-preserving variants prevent segmentation degradation and retain strong calibration performance. These results provide practical design principles for well-defined post-hoc calibration pipelines in semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [59] [SFKD: Spatial--Frequency Joint-Aware Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation via Multi-Level Wavelet Spectral Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01906)
*Cuipeng Wang,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SFKD框架，通过空间-频率联合感知的异构知识蒸馏，利用小波变换和傅里叶表示的互补特性来有效传递异构模型间的知识


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法主要关注同构模型（如CNN到CNN），忽略了异构模型间知识转移的灵活性和潜力。由于异构模型间固有的归纳偏置差异导致空间分布不一致，先前方法常常弱化或丢弃异构表示中的空间信息，而空间信息编码了可转移的全局结构语义和架构特定的局部细节，不应被直接忽略

Method: 提出空间-频率联合感知的异构知识蒸馏框架（SFKD）：1）利用小波变换的空间局部性和傅里叶表示在表征全局能量分布方面的互补特性；2）应用多级离散小波变换显式解耦空间信息；3）通过双流双阶段细化模块进一步精炼小波子带；4）结合高斯滤波的频率损失选择性地捕获信息丰富的全局信息

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，针对同构和异构模型进行了广泛实验，证明了该方法的优越性

Conclusion: SFKD框架能够更好地利用异构表示中编码的空间信息，有效解决了异构知识蒸馏中的空间分布不一致问题，显著提升了知识转移效果

Abstract: Most existing knowledge distillation methods focus on homogeneous models (e.g., CNN-to-CNN), thereby overlooking the flexibility and potential of knowledge transfer across heterogeneous models. Due to intrinsic inductive bias discrepancies between heterogeneous models that cause spatial distribution inconsistencies, prior heterogeneous distillation methods often weaken or discard spatial information in heterogeneous representations. However, the spatial information in representations often encodes transferable global structural semantics as well as architecture-specific local details, and therefore should not be directly ignored. To better leverage the spatial information encoded in heterogeneous representations, we propose a Spatial-Frequency Joint-Aware Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation framework (SFKD). By leveraging the complementary properties of wavelet transform spatial locality and Fourier representations in characterizing global energy distributions, we first apply multi-level discrete wavelet transform to explicitly decouple spatial information. The resulting wavelet sub-bands are further refined by a dual-stream dual-stage refinement module, and finally combined with a Gaussian-filtered frequency loss to selectively capture informative global information. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets under both homogeneous and heterogeneous models demonstrate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [60] [Towards Real-World Ultrasound Understanding: Large Vision-Language Models from Multi-Image Examinations with Long-Form Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01908)
*Bingcong Yan,Chunlei Li,Jingliang Hu,Yilei Shi,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Lichao Mou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过构建包含170万张真实世界超声检查的大规模数据集，采用简单的LoRA微调方法，证明了数据规模和临床数据对齐对于实现超声影像理解的关键作用，超越了先前更复杂的管道设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大视觉语言模型在医学影像任务中表现出色，但由于超声检查固有的复杂性和变异性，其在超声领域的应用仍然有限。研究者重新审视了实现真实世界超声理解真正需要的关键因素。

Method: 构建了包含150万次超声检查、1770万张图像的大规模数据集，涵盖多器官，并包含未经过滤的临床报告。关键在于按检查级别组织数据，将多张图像与其对应报告对齐以反映真实临床工作流程。然后使用低秩适应(LoRA)对标准大视觉语言模型进行微调，没有任务特定修改。

Result: 这种简单的方法在多种超声理解任务上表现出色，超越了先前采用更复杂管道设计的方法。此外，模型和数据规模分析提供了关于规模在超声大视觉语言模型中作用的洞察。

Conclusion: 数据规模和临床数据对齐是实现真实世界超声理解的关键因素，而非复杂的架构或训练策略。按检查级别组织数据以反映临床工作流程对于模型性能至关重要。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance across many medical imaging tasks, yet their application to ultrasound remains limited due to its inherent complexity and variability. In this work, we revisit what is truly needed to enable real-world ultrasound understanding. Instead of introducing complex architectures or elaborate training strategies, we show that data scale and clinically faithful data alignment are the key factors. We construct a large-scale dataset of 1.5M real-world ultrasound examinations, containing 17.7M images, multi-organ coverage, and paired uncurated clinical reports. Crucially, we organize the data at the examination level, aligning multiple images with their corresponding reports to reflect real clinical workflows. We then fine-tune a standard LVLM using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) on this dataset without task-specific modifications. Surprisingly, this simple recipe already leads to strong performance across diverse ultrasound understanding tasks, outperforming prior methods designed with more complex pipelines. Beyond these results, we present model and data scaling analyses that provide insights into the role of scale in ultrasound LVLMs.

</details>


### [61] [Robust Image Processing Techniques for Construction Environment Monitoring Using Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01915)
*Seunghee Yun,Geonmo Yang,Juhui Lee,Changbeom Park,Jeahyung Choi,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于水下机器人施工环境监测的鲁棒图像处理框架，通过建模前向散射和真实海洋雪模式来生成合成数据，重新训练现有网络并应用轻量后处理，在真实数据上实现了视觉质量和指标得分的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实水下图像除了吸收和背向散射外，还受到深度相关的前向散射模糊和粒子退化（如海洋雪）的严重影响，这些因素在实际海洋环境中造成了复杂的图像退化问题。传统的处理方法未能充分建模这些真实退化模式，导致合成数据与真实数据之间存在较大差距，限制了水下机器人监测系统的实用性能。

Method: 提出分阶段处理流水线：1）通过深度感知前向散射建模背景退化；2）使用从真实图像中提取的现实海洋雪模式建模前景退化。生成的合成数据用于在不修改架构的情况下重新训练现有的Joint-ID网络。此外，应用轻量级后处理方案来增强对比度和结构清晰度。

Result: 在韩国沿海环境收集的真实水下数据集上进行了实验，结果显示在视觉质量和UIQM得分方面均有持续改进。前向散射和现实粒子效应的显式建模有效缩小了合成数据与真实数据之间的差距。

Conclusion: 显式建模前向散射和现实粒子效应有效减少了合成与真实数据之间的差距，并提高了在实际水下机器人操作中的适用性。该方法通过更真实的退化建模提升了现有网络在实际环境中的性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a robust image processing framework for underwater robot-based construction environment monitoring, targeting complex degradations observed in real marine environments. Unlike conventional approaches that mainly consider absorption and backscattering, real underwater imagery is strongly affected by depth-dependent forward scattering blur and particle-induced degradations such as marine snow. To address this, we introduce a staged processing pipeline that sequentially models background degradation via depth-aware forward scattering and foreground degradation using realistic marine snow patterns extracted from real images. The resulting synthetic data are used to retrain an existing Joint-ID network without modifying its architecture, enabling an isolated evaluation of dataset realism. In addition, a lightweight post-processing scheme is applied to enhance contrast and structural clarity. Experiments on real underwater datasets collected in Korean coastal environments demonstrate consistent improvements in visual quality and UIQM scores. The results indicate that explicitly modeling forward scattering and realistic particle effects effectively reduces the synthetic-to-real gap and improves practical applicability in real-world underwater robotic operations.

</details>


### [62] [Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization for Bandwidth-Efficient Collaborative 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01928)
*Feng Li,Chaokun Zhang,Gong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VQSOP框架，通过稀疏感知向量量化和双分支自适应空间细化模块，在显著降低通信开销的同时实现高性能的3D语义占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知需要在感知增益和通信开销之间权衡，特别是对于依赖细粒度空间结构的3D语义占用预测。现有方法要么将3D特征压缩为2D导致空间信息损失，要么传输密集3D表示阻碍实际部署。

Method: 提出带宽高效的协同向量量化语义占用预测(VQSOP)框架：1) 稀疏感知向量量化(SAVQ)机制，利用3D场景稀疏性紧凑编码信息丰富区域；2) 双分支自适应空间细化(ASR)模块，动态融合局部高频细节与广泛上下文语义。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在保持完整几何上下文的同时，将通信量减少高达82倍，并达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: VQSOP框架通过创新的稀疏编码和自适应融合机制，有效解决了协同感知中的带宽效率问题，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends single-agent perception by enabling multiple vehicles to exchange complementary perceptual information. However, it introduces an inherent trade-off between perception gain and communication overhead, which is particularly severe for 3D semantic occupancy prediction that relies on fine-grained spatial structures. Existing methods typically compress 3D features into 2D, causing severe spatial information loss, or transmit dense 3D representations, hindering real-world deployment. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bandwidth-efficient collaborative Vector Quantization Semantic Occupancy Prediction (VQSOP) framework. VQSOP employs a Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization (SAVQ) mechanism that exploits 3D scene sparsity to compactly encode informative regions, drastically reducing communication overhead while preserving complete geometric context. Furthermore, to enhance structural consistency and feature continuity, we design a Dual-Branch Adaptive Spatial Refinement (ASR) module that dynamically fuses local high-frequency details with broad contextual semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing communication volume by up to 82x.

</details>


### [63] [ESC: Emotional Self-Correction for Reliable Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02089)
*Tien-Huy Nguyen,Minh-Nhat Nguyen,Nguyen Nhat Huy,Hung Viet Nguyen,Huy Nguyen Minh Nhat,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen,Hoang M. Le,Dat Nguyen,Phat Kim Huynh,Min Xu,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ESC：一种无需训练的情感触发自校正框架，通过情感信号激活视觉语言模型的潜在自校正能力，提高模型可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多模态任务上表现优异，但存在不可靠推理的脆弱性。现有自校正方法通常需要后训练或精心设计的反馈，计算成本高昂。研究团队发现情感信号可以作为有效的自校正触发器，促使模型进行更谨慎和反思的推理

Method: 提出ESC（情感自校正）框架，无需额外训练。引入外部验证器检测可能错误的初始响应，注入情感反馈鼓励模型反思并产生更好的修正响应。这是一个训练免费的自校正方法

Result: 在安全、幻觉、视觉中心感知和多模态推理基准上的广泛实验表明，ESC能持续提高可靠性，同时保持整体模型实用性。情感不仅可作为识别能力，还可作为视觉语言模型可扩展自校正的实际控制信号

Conclusion: ESC为可靠、类人、情感集成的研究方向奠定了坚实基础。情感可以激活视觉语言模型的潜在自校正行为，无需额外训练即可实现可扩展的可靠性提升

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to unreliable reasoning. Existing self-correction methods mitigate these issues but typically rely on post-training or carefully engineered feedback, incurring high computational cost. In this work, we revisit this challenge through the lens of emotional cues, asking whether they can activate latent self-correction behaviors in VLMs without additional training. \textbf{We find that emotional signals serve as an effective trigger for self-correction, encouraging more cautious and reflective reasoning}. Motivated by this finding, we propose \escabstract (\textbf{\underline{E}}motional \textbf{\underline{S}}elf-\textbf{\underline{C}}orrection), a training-free self-correction framework. ESC introduces an external verifier that detects potentially incorrect initial responses and injects emotional feedback to encourage model to reflect, and produce a better revised response without additional training. Extensive experiments across safety, hallucination, vision-centric perception, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that ESC consistently improves reliability while preserving overall model utility. These results suggest that emotion can function not only as an ability to be recognized, but also as a practical control signal for scalable self-correction in VLMs. \textbf{We therefore believe that ESC provides a strong foundation for a new reliable human-like, emotion-integrated research direction.} Our project is publicly available at \textcolor{red}{https://genai4e.github.io/ESC/}.

</details>


### [64] [LiZAD: A Lightweight Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection Framework for Industrial Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01949)
*Uzair Khan,Luigi Capogrosso,Muhammad Aqeel,Francesco Setti,Michele Magno,Marco Cristani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiZAD是一个轻量级零样本异常检测框架，专为边缘设备设计，通过结合DINOv3的视觉特征和MobileCLIP2的文本嵌入，在保持检测性能的同时大幅降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代高吞吐量工业生产线上，产品配置和视觉特征频繁变化，为每个新场景收集和标注数据不切实际。虽然零样本异常检测方法很适用，但现有方法计算密集，不适合资源受限的边缘设备部署。

Method: 提出LiZAD框架：结合DINOv3的密集空间感知视觉特征（用于像素级定位）和MobileCLIP2的高效计算文本嵌入，通过低内存可训练投影头将这些特征映射到共享潜在空间。

Result: 相比6个SOTA ZSAD模型，LiZAD平均内存减少61.5%，参数减少74.6%，延迟加速3.02倍。平均P-AUROC仅比最佳SOTA模型下降6.4%。成功部署于NVIDIA Jetson NX和AGX边缘设备，并在真实产线测试。

Conclusion: LiZAD在显著降低计算和内存成本的同时保持了竞争力的异常检测性能，为边缘设备上的实时零样本异常检测提供了实用解决方案，已在真实工业环境中验证。

Abstract: In modern high-throughput industrial production lines, product configurations and visual characteristics frequently change, making it impractical to collect and annotate data for every new scenario. This dynamic setting makes Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection (ZSAD) particularly suitable, as it enables defect detection without requiring training on target-specific samples. Although recent ZSAD approaches show promising results, they are computationally intensive and thus unsuitable for deployment on resource-constrained devices. We propose LiZAD: a lightweight framework designed for real-time ZSAD specifically tailored for use on edge devices. The proposed approach pairs the dense and spatially aware visual features of DINOv3, crucial for precise pixel-level localization, with the highly computationally efficient text embeddings of MobileCLIP2. These features are then mapped into a shared latent space via low-memory trainable projection heads. Compared to six state-of-the-art ZSAD models, LiZAD achieves an average memory reduction of 61.5%, a parameter reduction of 74.6%, and a speedup of 3.02x in terms of latency. Despite substantial reductions in computational and memory costs, our approach maintains competitive anomaly detection performance, dropping the average P-AUROC by just 6.4% relative to the best state-of-the-art model across the VisA, BTAD, MPDD, and MVTec-AD datasets. Finally, it is successfully deployed on the NVIDIA Jetson NX and Jetson AGX edge devices and tested on the real production line of the Industrial Computer Engineering Laboratory (ICE Lab) at the University of Verona. The code is available at https://github.com/intelligolabs/LiZAD.

</details>


### [65] [Personalized 4D Whole-Heart Mesh Reconstruction from Cine MRI via Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling and Differentiable Contour Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01952)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Dongcheng Cang,Xiaohan Yuan,Mark YY Chan,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端框架，从稀疏多视角2D电影MRI序列重建时间分辨的全心脏网格，通过可微分轮廓渲染器和多尺度时间建模模块实现高精度和时序一致的心脏4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏电影MRI准确重建4D全心脏网格对于创建心脏数字孪生至关重要，但现有方法常依赖中间轮廓拟合，只能重建静态、单相或部分心脏几何形状，无法捕捉全腔室动态。

Method: 1) 学习图像到网格的端到端映射；2) 引入受Beer-Lambert衰减原理启发的可微分轮廓渲染器，通过轮廓投影损失实现解剖感知的3D+t网格形变监督；3) 提出多尺度时间建模模块，整合全局周期级动态和局部帧间一致性以生成平滑网格轨迹。

Result: 全心脏平均绝对误差1.68±0.31毫米，运动抖动0.77±0.17毫米/帧³，优于现有方法，重建误差更低，运动平滑度显著改善，并改善了2D轮廓在多视图的对齐，支持下游电生理仿真概念验证。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了从稀疏多视角2D电影MRI的端到端时间分辨全心脏网格重建，通过解剖感知监督和多尺度时间建模实现了高精度和平滑的心脏动力学重建，为心脏数字孪生创建提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Accurate 4D whole-heart mesh reconstruction from sparse cine MRI is critical for creating cardiac digital twins, but remains challenging due to limited 2D slice coverage and the complex coupling between cardiac shape and motion. Existing methods often rely on intermediate contour fitting and typically reconstruct static, single-phase, or partial cardiac geometries, limiting their ability to capture full-chamber dynamics. We propose a novel end-to-end framework for reconstructing temporally resolved whole-heart meshes from multi-view 2D cine MRI sequences by learning an image-to-mesh mapping. The framework incorporates a differentiable contour renderer inspired by the Beer-Lambert attenuation principle, enabling anatomy-aware supervision of 3D+t mesh deformation through contour-based projection losses. To improve temporal consistency across the cardiac cycle, we further introduce a multi-scale temporal modeling module that integrates global cycle-level dynamics with local inter-frame coherence to generate smooth and physiologically plausible mesh trajectories. The proposed method achieved a whole-heart mean absolute error of 1.68 $\pm$ 0.31 mm and a motion jitter of 0.77 $\pm$ 0.17 $\mathrm{mm}/\mathrm{frame}^{3}$, outperforming existing methods with lower reconstruction error and substantially improved motion smoothness. It also improved 2D contour alignment across multiple cine MRI views and supported downstream proof-of-concept electrophysiological simulation. The code will be released publicly upon acceptance of the manuscript for publication.

</details>


### [66] [NeoMap: Training-free Novel-View Synthesis from Single Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01962)
*Jinxi Li,Tianyi Zhang,Yafei Yang,Zihui Zhang,Peng Huang,Koon Wing Macgyver Lin,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeoMap是一个无需训练的框架，用于从预训练视频模型中定位高质量、视角一致的新视角合成解决方案，通过收敛流形交替投影迭代优化初始噪声，在多个标准基准上取得最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设预训练视频模型缺乏原生新视角合成能力，通过相机条件、任务特定微调或逐步硬去噪引导强制视角对齐，往往产生伪影并损害全局场景一致性。

Method: 提出NeoMap框架，核心机制是通过收敛流形交替投影迭代优化初始噪声，在预训练模型学习的自然视频数据流形中定位最佳的新视角合成解决方案，无需额外训练。

Result: 在Tanks-and-Temples、LLFF和DAVIS三个标准新视角合成基准测试中显著优于所有现有方法，实现了最先进的生成保真度和顶级的视角一致性。

Conclusion: NeoMap通过揭示预训练视频模型中已编码的新视角合成能力，提供了一种无需训练的高质量新视角视频合成框架，突破了现有方法在视角对齐和场景一致性方面的限制。

Abstract: We study the challenging problem of novel view video synthesis from single images or monocular videos. Existing methods, which operate under the assumption that pre-trained video models lack native novel view synthesis capability and enforce view alignment via camera conditioning, task-specific fine-tuning, or stepwise hard denoising guidance, often suffer from artifacts and compromised global scene consistency. In this paper, we introduce NeoMap, a novel training-free framework designed to locate high-fidelity, view-consistent novel view solutions from general pre-trained video models. The key to our approach is the core insight that promising novel view solutions are inherently encoded within the natural video data manifold learned by pre-trained models, and the core challenge is simply to locate this optimal solution. We solve this via our core mechanism: convergent manifold alternating projection iterations that optimize the initial noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoMap significantly outperforms all existing methods across 3 standard novel view synthesis benchmarks, including the challenging Tanks-and-Temples, LLFF and DAVIS datasets, achieving state-of-the-art generation fidelity and top-tier view consistency.

</details>


### [67] [Assessing VLM Reliability for Medical Image Quality Evaluation Under Corruption and Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01973)
*Sofiane Ouaari,Kevin Vorwalder,Nico Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估16个视觉语言模型在医疗图像质量评估任务中的表现，发现像素化腐败对评分影响最大，文本属性（如机构声望）会显著改变质量评分，揭示了当前VLM在医疗应用中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在医疗任务中应用日益广泛，医疗图像质量评估对诊断准确性和患者安全至关重要。虽然自动化MIQA可能减少工作量，但需要了解VLM在真实世界条件下（如图像退化或文本上下文影响）的行为表现。

Method: 使用MediMeta-C数据集对16个VLM进行零样本评估，涵盖七种腐败类型（像素化、亮度等）和五个严重级别。评估模型对退化模式的敏感性、腐败对嵌入几何的影响，以及文本属性（人口统计学、专业知识、基础设施、机构）对评分的影响。

Result: 像素化导致评分最大下降（平均-20.58%，OCT最高-34.4%），亮度影响有限（-0.81%）。嵌入位移与评分变化相关。同族模型间相关性0.67-0.83。文本属性显著影响评分：机构声望提升+17.15%，设备年龄降低-14.7%。最大变化为+95.62%（InternVL-8B）和-37.7%（MedGemma）。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在医疗图像质量评估中存在局限性：像素化（隐私保护技术）会降低性能，表明患者隐私与可靠性之间的权衡；对上下文元数据的敏感性表明有限客观性，元数据成为隐私和偏见来源；隐私保护和客观质量评估是相关需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied in medical tasks such as pathology description, report generation, and visual question answering. Medical Image Quality Assessment (MIQA) supports diagnostic accuracy and patient safety by determining whether images meet the standards required for clinical decision-making. Automating MIQA with VLMs may reduce workload, but their behavior under real-world conditions, where images may be degraded or textual context may affect judgments, should be further explored before deployment. We benchmark VLMs on medical image quality using the MediMeta-C dataset zero-shot across seven corruption types and five severity levels. We evaluate sensitivity to degradation patterns, the effect of corruptions on embedding geometry, and whether textual attributes (demographics, expertise, infrastructure, institution) alter scores. Across 16 VLMs and seven modalities, pixelation produced the largest score reductions (mean -20.58%, up to -34.4% for OCT), whereas brightness had limited effect (-0.81%). Embedding displacement was associated with score changes. Same-family models showed correlations of 0.67-0.83; some produced increases up to +31% for corrupted mammography. Textual attributes affected scores: institutional prestige raised them +17.15%, and equipment age lowered them -14.7%. The largest changes were +95.62% (InternVL-8B) and -37.7% (MedGemma). Current VLMs show limitations for medical image quality assessment. Pixelation, a privacy-preserving transformation, reduces performance, indicating a trade-off between patient privacy and reliability. Sensitivity to contextual metadata indicates limited objectivity and marks metadata as a privacy and bias source. Privacy protection and objective quality assessment are related requirements for use.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的机制可解释性方法，用于解决CLIP模型中文本攻击的鲁棒性问题，通过采样解释和电路挖掘识别对词汇信息编码过度的ViT组件，并通过简单的干预策略显著提升目标分类和视觉问答任务的抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型作为大多数大型视觉语言模型的基础视觉编码器，存在一个关键但未被充分探索的失效模式：图像中出现的无关文本会混淆视觉表示，使其偏向词汇意义而非真实视觉语义。这种被称为文本攻击的鲁棒性问题对自动驾驶等安全关键应用构成重大风险。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的机制可解释性方法，提供隐藏状态表示的采样解释，并通过概率分析和电路挖掘，定量归因各个注意力头对语义与词汇信息的关注程度。该方法识别出Vision Transformer中过度编码词汇信息的特定组件。

Result: 实验表明，直接对识别出的电路应用简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）而无需额外训练，能显著提升对文本攻击的鲁棒性。在对象分类任务中，该方法优于监督学习和无需训练的防御方法。将干预应用于多个先进LVLMs的视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench上受到文本攻击干扰的视觉问答任务准确率获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 该机制可解释性方法不仅有效解决了CLIP模型中的文本攻击鲁棒性问题，而且展现了良好的泛化能力。通过识别机制根源并应用针对性干预，能够在不重新训练的情况下显著提升模型对视觉语义的专注度，为安全关键应用提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [69] [MolSight: A Graph-Aware Vision-Language Model for Unified Chemical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01982)
*Wenda Wang,Yihan Tong,Yuwei Hu,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MolSight是一个图感知的视觉语言模型框架，通过整合分子拓扑信息和视觉-化学语义对齐来增强对分子图像的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子大语言模型(LLMs)虽然为分子结构和功能理解提供了统一框架，但难以充分捕捉分子结构的视觉表示。现有的分子视觉语言模型(VLMs)在结构对齐和拓扑建模方面存在局限，影响了对分子的准确理解。

Method: 提出MolSight框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 分子拓扑模块，将化学键邻接信息注入视觉标记；2) 分子对齐模块，将视觉特征与化学符号语义对齐。

Result: MolSight在多个化学视觉理解任务中显著优于现有的VLMs、分子LLMs和专业工具，实现了分子图像推理的新水平。

Conclusion: 通过整合图感知的拓扑建模和语义对齐，MolSight有效提升了分子视觉理解能力，为分子设计和药物发现等任务提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Using molecular large language models (LLMs) as a unified framework for understanding molecular structures and functions is emerging as a new trend in tasks such as molecular design and drug discovery. However, these models struggle to fully capture the visual representation of molecular structures, limiting their potential. While existing molecular vision-language models (VLMs) show promise, they still face challenges in structural alignment and lack the necessary topological modeling for accurate molecular understanding. To address this, we propose MolSight, a graph-aware vision-language model framework designed to enhance the understanding of molecular images by VLMs. MolSight integrates a Molecular Topology Module to inject chemical-bond adjacency information into vision tokens, and a Molecular Grounding Module to align visual features with chemical symbolic semantics. Our experiments demonstrate that MolSight significantly outperforms existing VLMs, molecular LLMs, and specialized tools across multiple chemical visual understanding tasks, achieving a new level of molecular image reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [Open-Weather Robust 3D Detection via Dual-Critic Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01983)
*Shuyao Li,Chuanxing Geng,Heyang Sun,Qiang Zhou,Jingjing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCDA框架，通过双判别器引导的扩散对齐方法，在无需配对数据或天气标签的情况下，实现开放场景下对抗恶劣天气的鲁棒3D目标检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
