Motivation: 领域增量变化检测面临结构性不匹配问题：标签空间固定而领域特征变化巨大。现有方法（如重播或正则化）难以扩展到长领域序列，导致知识退化或计算成本增加。模型难以跨领域保持稳定的空间变化表示。

Method: 提出双选择性增量网络（DSINet）：1) 选择性空间状态单元（S3U），利用Mamba的输入依赖性选择机制，在特征传播中保持稳定的空间变化结构同时过滤领域特异性变化；2) 浓度平衡蒸馏（CBD）策略，平衡硬度和置信度集中效应，确保可靠的概率质量分配，防止蒸馏过程中的过度平滑或模式崩溃。

Result: 实验结果表明，DSINet能够减轻长领域序列中的知识退化，同时保持状态空间模型的线性计算效率。该方法在跨领域保持稳定空间表示方面表现优异，防止了增量步骤中特征混淆的累积。

Conclusion: DSINet通过选择性空间状态单元和浓度平衡蒸馏机制的协同作用，解决了领域增量变化检测中的结构性不匹配问题，实现了跨领域的稳定学习动态，为长序列增量学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain-incremental change detection (DICD) continuously adapts models to new geographic domains while preserving prior knowledge. However, a structural mismatch exists: the label space remains fixed while domain characteristics vary drastically. Consequently, incremental models struggle to maintain stable spatial change representations across domains. Existing strategies, such as replay-based or regularization-based methods, often fail to scale to long domain sequences, leading to knowledge degradation or increased computational cost. We propose Dual-Selective Incremental Network (DSINet), a unified framework built on visual state space models. DSINet leverages Mamba's input-dependent selective mechanism through a selective spatial state unit (S3U). This unit preserves stable spatial change structures while filtering domain-specific variations during feature propagation. As a result, spatial representations remain stable across domains, preventing the accumulation of feature confusion over incremental steps. Additionally, we employ a concentration-balanced distillation (CBD) strategy to stabilize knowledge transfer across domains. It balances hardness and confidence concentration effects during incremental updates. This ensures reliable probability mass allocation and prevents over-smoothing or mode collapse during distillation. Together, these mechanisms maintain stable learning dynamics throughout incremental stages. Experimental results demonstrate that DSINet mitigates knowledge degradation across long domain sequences while maintaining the linear computational efficiency of state space models.

</details>


### [105] [Search-based Testing of Vision Language Models for In-Car Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02300)
*Lev Sorokin,Chen Yang,Ken E. Friedl,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ISU-Test：一种结合基于渲染的场景生成和基于搜索测试的自动化方法，用于评估车内场景理解系统，特别是视觉语言模型在车载环境中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在汽车领域中，车内场景理解（ISU）能够检测安全关键事件（如驾驶员分心），但行业采用的视觉语言模型（VLMs）可能生成不完整、错误或误导性的场景描述，需要系统化测试。收集真实车载数据成本高、难以扩展，特别是在早期设计阶段不可行。

Method: ISU-Test将测试框架化为优化问题，结合基于渲染的场景生成和基于搜索的测试。通过系统修改场景参数，生成多样化的车内场景并探索广泛的配置范围。

Result: 在两个案例研究（问答和字幕生成）中评估ISU-Test，结果显示该方法显著优于随机场景生成基线，实现了高达10倍的失败率和3.6倍的失败覆盖率。

Conclusion: ISU-Test提供了一种有效的自动化测试方法，能够系统评估车内场景理解系统，解决了真实数据收集困难的问题，并在工业原型和开源视觉语言模型上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In the automotive domain, in-car scene understanding (ISU) enables the detection of safety-critical events, such as driver distraction, and supports drivers or passengers by analyzing the in-car scene and adapting the environment (e.g., ambient lighting). The industry is increasingly exploring vision-language models (VLMs) to interpret camera-recorded in-car scenes and extract information for downstream reasoning tasks. However, VLMs may generate incomplete, erroneous, or misleading scene descriptions, highlighting the need for systematic testing. Collecting real in-vehicle data is costly, difficult to scale, and often infeasible, particularly in early design stages. In this paper, we present ISU-Test, an automated testing approach that combines rendering-based scene generation with search-based testing to evaluate ISU systems. By framing testing as an optimization problem and systematically modifying scene parameters, our method generates diverse in-car scenarios and explores a wide range of configurations. We evaluate ISU-Test on both an industrial prototype and open-source VLMs across two case studies: question answering and captioning, comparing against randomized scenario generation. Results show that ISU-Test significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 10 times higher failure rates and up to 3.6 times higher failure coverage.

</details>


### [106] [InvSplat: Inverse Feed-Forward Scene Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02301)
*Polina Karpikova,Wenjing Bian,Haofei Xu,Hendrik Lensch,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于前馈多视图重建的逆渲染框架，可直接预测具有内在材质属性的结构化3D高斯表示，实现几何和反射参数的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆渲染需要从图像中恢复3D几何和物理上有意义的材质属性。现有方法存在局限性：基于优化的方法虽然保真度高但需要昂贵的每场景拟合成本；基于图像空间的学习方法通常存在多视图不一致性，且缺乏用于稳定新视图渲染的显式3D表示。

Method: 提出了一个前馈多视图重建框架，直接预测结构化的3D高斯表示。每个高斯基元参数化为均值、法线、不透明度、旋转、尺度、反照率、金属度和粗糙度。模型将材质估计网络的先验与多视图3D重建主干网络集成，在单次前向传播中联合预测几何和反射参数。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，与2D基线相比具有更好的多视图一致性、准确的材质恢复和稳定的新视图渲染。该表示进一步支持基于物理的重光照，并比现有的基于RGB的前馈重建方法能更真实地建模视点相关效应。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了从图像到具有物理意义的材质属性的3D表示的端到端前馈逆渲染，解决了现有方法在效率和一致性方面的挑战，为逆渲染任务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to recover both 3D geometry and physically meaningful material properties from images, enabling applications such as relighting and novel view synthesis. Optimization-based methods achieve high fidelity but require costly per-scene fitting, while image-space learning-based approaches often suffer from multi-view inconsistencies and lack an explicit 3D representation for stable novel view rendering. We present a feed-forward multi-view reconstruction framework for inverse rendering that directly predicts a structured 3D Gaussian representation with intrinsic material attributes. Each Gaussian primitive is parameterized by mean, normal, opacity, rotation, scale, albedo, metallic, and roughness, enabling a disentangled and physically grounded scene representation. Our model integrates priors from a material estimation network with a multi-view 3D reconstruction backbone, allowing joint prediction of geometry and reflectance parameters in a single forward pass. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate improved multi-view consistency compared to 2D baselines, accurate material recovery, and stable novel view rendering. Our representation further supports physically-based relighting and more faithful modeling of view-dependent effects compared to existing RGB-based feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project webpage is: $\href{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}{\text{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}}$.

</details>


### [107] [NEvo: Neural-Guided Evolutionary Video Synthesis for Dynamic Visual Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02317)
*Yingtian Tang,Sogand Salehi,Ming Zhou,Amir Zamir,Leyla Isik,Martin Schrimpf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究者开发了一种神经引导的视频合成框架，通过动态编码模型和结构化提示空间的进化搜索，生成针对大脑视觉皮层特定区域优化的刺激视频，用于探索动态视觉处理的选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有的脑编码模型能够合成最优刺激来探测大脑区域的选择性，但这些工作主要局限于静态图像，对动态视觉处理的探索不足。大脑通过层次化组织的功能专门化区域处理动态视觉输入，需要开发新的方法来研究动态视觉处理机制。

Method: 提出了一种神经引导的视频合成框架：1) 使用动态编码模型预测体素级对视频输入的反应；2) 在结构化提示空间上进行进化搜索；3) 通过最大化目标感兴趣区域（ROI）的预测活动来生成超激活动态刺激；4) 应用搜索灯分析探索大脑区域的功能特性。

Result: 1) 合成的视频刺激持续优于手工制作的定位视频；2) 恢复了腹侧、背侧和外侧通路的已知选择性；3) 揭示了不同区域对时间动态敏感性的系统差异；4) 搜索灯分析发现外侧通路对社会动态特征的复杂性逐渐增加，并通过合成的抽象、非自然刺激得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对动态视觉选择性的硅内探索，为体内实验提供了新的预测，推动了动态视觉处理研究的进展，为理解大脑如何处理动态视觉信息提供了新的工具和见解。

Abstract: The human brain processes dynamic visual input through hierarchically organized, functionally specialized regions. While recent in silico brain encoding models can synthesize optimal stimuli to probe selectivity in different brain regions, prior work has been largely limited to static images, leaving dynamic visual processing underexplored. We introduce a novel neural-guided video synthesis framework that generates stimuli optimized for target brain regions across visual cortex. Our method performs evolutionary search over a structured prompt space, guided by a dynamic encoding model that predicts voxel-level responses to video inputs. By maximizing predicted activity for a target ROI, the framework efficiently discovers hyper-activating dynamic stimuli that consistently surpass handcrafted localizer videos. The synthesized videos recover known selectivities across ventral, dorsal, and lateral pathways, and further reveal systematic differences in sensitivity to temporal dynamics. A searchlight analysis provides new insight into the progression toward increasingly complex social-dynamic features along the lateral stream, further supported by probing with synthesized abstract, non-naturalistic stimuli. Taken together, our framework enables in silico exploration of dynamic visual selectivity, with new predictions for in vivo experiments

</details>


### [108] [GAP-GDRNet: Geometry-Aware Monocular Visual Pose Sensing on a Single-Target Synthetic Spacecraft Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02360)
*Yonglong Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GAP-GDRNet是一个用于单目RGB图像6D姿态感知的几何感知注意力增强框架，改进了GDR-Net方法，通过两个注意力模块（AFR和PGSA）增强航天器结构的全局特征和局部纹理线索，提高在纹理弱、遮挡等挑战性场景下的姿态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作交会和在轨服务中，单目相对姿态感知是一个核心感知问题。航天器图像通常面临表面纹理弱、薄附件、光照变化和部分遮挡等挑战，导致只能获取稀疏且不稳定的几何证据，因此需要更鲁棒的姿态估计方法。

Method: 方法基于GDR-Net的几何引导直接回归范式，引入了两个关键改进：1) 在密集几何预测之前添加注意力特征细化模块（AFR），增强全局航天器结构和局部弱纹理线索；2) 在Patch-PnP中插入补丁级几何自注意力模块（PGSA），在最终姿态回归前关联降采样后的几何补丁。使用Blender-based标注流程提供训练数据，包括目标掩码、可见区域掩码、密集模型坐标图、相机内参和6D姿态标签。

Result: 论文提出GAP-GDRNet框架，通过注意力机制增强几何特征表示，在稀疏几何证据条件下提高了单目6D姿态估计的鲁棒性和准确性。具体量化结果在论文正文中呈现。

Conclusion: GAP-GDRNet通过结合几何引导和注意力机制，有效解决了航天器图像中纹理弱、遮挡等挑战，为单目相对姿态感知提供了更可靠的解决方案，适用于非合作交会和在轨服务等空间应用。

Abstract: Monocular relative pose sensing is a central perception problem in non-cooperative rendezvous and on-orbit servicing. In spacecraft images, however, weak surface texture, thin appendages, illumination changes, and partial occlusion often leave only sparse and unstable geometric evidence. This article presents GAP-GDRNet, a geometry-aware attention-enhanced framework for monocular RGB-based 6D pose sensing. The method follows the geometry-guided direct regression paradigm of GDR-Net and modifies two points in the pipeline: an attention-based feature refinement (AFR) module is placed before dense geometric prediction, and a patch-level geometric self-attention (PGSA) module is inserted into Patch-PnP. AFR reinforces global spacecraft structure together with local weak-texture cues; PGSA then relates downsampled geometric patches before final pose regression. A Blender-based annotation process supplies target masks, visible-region masks, dense model-coordinate maps, camera intrinsics, and 6D pose labels for supervised training.

</details>


### [109] [VisionAId: An Offline-First Multimodal Android Assistant for People with Visual Impairment, Featuring Personalized Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02371)
*Cristian-Gabriel Florea,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionAId是一个将智能手机转变为实时视觉助手的Android应用，通过六个本地深度学习模型和可选云LLM，为视障人士提供障碍物避让、物品定位、人脸识别等功能，并采用少样本学习进行个人物品识别


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球有超过2.85亿视障人士，他们在日常生活中面临诸多障碍，如避让障碍物、寻找个人物品、识别人脸等。现有辅助应用通常限于预定义类别识别、依赖云连接或需要专用硬件，无法满足实际需求

Method: 1) 开发基于Android的VisionAId应用，集成六个本地深度学习模型：度量单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉和面部嵌入、人脸检测、定制纸币检测器，全部通过ONNX Runtime运行
2) 可选使用Google Gemini Flash进行场景描述和自动对象标注
3) 创新的少样本个人物品管道：用户从多个角度拍摄物品照片，系统后在环境中定位该特定实例
4) 多模态反馈：增强现实标记、空间音频、距离比例触觉反馈、罗马尼亚语语音合成、语音命令、振动

Result: 1) 在三星Galaxy S21 Ultra上，INT8量化将深度估计延迟从约1200ms降至约491ms
2) 定制纸币检测器达到mAP@50为0.986的准确率
3) 度量深度在3米范围内校准误差低于1厘米
4) 系统提供实时、完全本地的视觉辅助功能

Conclusion: VisionAId成功开发了一个完全本地运行的视觉辅助系统，通过创新的少样本个人物品识别和多模态反馈机制，为视障人士提供了实用的日常帮助，解决了现有解决方案在本地处理能力、个性化识别和硬件依赖性方面的限制

Abstract: Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02372)
*Federico Lincetto,Gianluca Agresti,Mattia Rossi,Piergiorgio Sartor,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPoILeR：一种通过多模态预训练学习模态间相关性，从而仅使用RGB图像就能准确渲染红外、偏振和多光谱等非常规模态的神经渲染方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染技术扩展到多模态（如多光谱、红外或偏振数据）时，需要昂贵的传感器和校准设备来为每个新场景捕获多模态帧，这限制了应用范围和可行性。

Method: 提出SPoILeR（光谱和偏振隐式学习表示）方法，包括两个阶段：1) 多模态预训练阶段，学习不同模态间的相互相关性；2) 微调阶段，仅使用RGB图像作为监督，预测非常规模态的准确渲染。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够准确渲染红外、偏振和多光谱帧，即使对于没有提供这些类型传感器输入样本的场景也能生成多视角一致的渲染结果。

Conclusion: SPoILeR方法通过预训练学习模态相关性，仅需RGB图像或极少多模态样本就能渲染非常规模态，降低了多模态渲染的硬件成本和数据获取难度，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.

</details>


### [111] [Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02375)
*Lan Feng,Wuyang Li,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出改进的表示分布匹配（iRDM）方法，通过匹配生成图像与参考图像在预训练编码器下的特征分布来训练单步图像生成器，在ImageNet上达到单步生成器的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的表示分布匹配（RDM）方法在过去十年中未能训练出令人信服的生成器，需要系统地探索设计空间以改善性能。研究者旨在理解如何有效匹配生成图像和参考图像在冻结预训练编码器下的特征分布。

Method: 系统探索RDM的两个设计轴：分布比较方式和比较所在的表示空间。提出三个关键发现：1）正确估计时MMD成为强大且可扩展的目标；2）生成批次大小需超过2048；3）匹配需要平衡的编码器电池以防止"博弈"。最终提出iRDM，使用基于14个编码器的切片Wasserstein距离（SW_r14）作为评估指标。

Result: iRDM在ImageNet上创下单步生成器的最先进水平（SW_r14 1.30）。PickScore评估显示71.2%的匹配样本优于先前最佳单步生成器。将四步FLUX.2后训练为单步生成器，在GenEval（0.826 vs 0.794）和PickScore（22.76 vs 22.58）上均超过原始四步版本，仅需90 H200 GPU小时。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析RDM设计空间，论文展示了MMD在正确实现下能够训练高质量单步生成器，并提出iRDM作为有效的单步图像生成方法，在质量和效率上都达到最先进水平。

Abstract: We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.

</details>


### [112] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-II: Representational Similarity Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02386)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TGO-II框架分析Vision Transformer训练过程中表示几何的演化，发现表示复杂性增加、层间专门化以及token交互结构持续存在，表明ViTs通过保持强token交互结构的同时进行丰富的表示变换来增加表示复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在计算机视觉和语言应用中取得了显著成功，但其训练过程中内部表示几何的演化仍未得到充分理解。现有分析主要关注注意力机制和下游性能，而表示几何的演化基本未被探索。

Method: 提出了Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II)表示几何分析框架，用于研究Transformer在监督训练过程中表示如何演化。使用Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)、Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA)、Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID)和token协方差分析来分析Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16)表示。

Result: 1. CKA和SVCCA在训练过程中持续下降，表明跨Transformer层的表示专门化程度增加。2. 内在维度先持续增加后稳定，表明表示流形逐渐扩展到更大的局部可访问自由度集合。3. token协方差和耦合分析显示强token交互结构在整个训练过程中持续存在，挑战了表示复杂性增加主要源于token独立性逐渐增加的观点。

Conclusion: 表示复杂性和层专门化在训练过程中同时出现。流形扩展似乎在没有token解耦的情况下发生。这些观察激发了一个新假设：Vision Transformers通过逐步丰富的变换增加表示复杂性，同时在学习过程中保持强token交互结构。

Abstract: While Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success across computer vision and language applications, the geometric evolution of their internal representations throughout training remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses primarily focus on attention mechanisms and downstream performance, leaving the evolution of representation geometry largely unexplored. In this work, we present Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II), a representation geometry analysis framework designed to investigate how Transformer representations evolve during supervised training. TGO-II analyzes Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16) representations using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA), Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID), and token covariance analysis. Our experiments reveal three key observations. First, both CKA and SVCCA progressively decrease throughout training, indicating increasing representational specialization across Transformer layers. Second, intrinsic dimensionality consistently increases before stabilizing, suggesting progressive expansion of the representation manifold into a larger set of locally accessible degrees of freedom. Third, token covariance and coupling analyses demonstrate that strong token interaction structure persists throughout training, challenging the hypothesis that increasing representational complexity arises primarily from progressive token independence. These findings suggest that representation complexity and layer specialization emerge simultaneously during training. Manifold expansion appears to occur without token decoupling. Together, these observations motivate a new hypothesis in which Vision Transformers increase representational complexity through progressively richer transformations while preserving strong token interaction structure during learning.

</details>


### [113] [Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02402)
*Nick Stracke,Kolja Bauer,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Miguel Angel Bautista,Josh Susskind,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了视觉概念集合推理（VICIS）任务，旨在评估模型从一组示例图像中推断共享概念并应用于新输入的能力。针对现有视觉语言模型在此任务上的不足，作者提出了新的训练框架和架构，在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet数据上取得了更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽然能够遵循复杂的文本指令，但难以从纯视觉上下文进行推理。特别是，现有模型无法从一组示例图像中推断共享概念并将其应用于新输入。作者针对这一局限性，提出了VICIS任务来评估模型的这种能力。

Method: 作者提出了一个训练框架和架构，能够从图像集合中学习推断视觉概念，并从查询图像中提取概念特定的嵌入。该方法通过结合视觉上下文理解和概念推理能力，解决了现有模型的问题。

Result: 在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet大规模数据上的实验表明，该模型比现有最先进的视觉语言模型表现更好，生成的输出更准确且多样化。模型能够泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如草图），显示出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了视觉概念集合推理的关键挑战，提出的方法和架构在VICIS任务上显著优于现有方法，展现了从纯视觉上下文中学习和应用概念的强大能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.

</details>


### [114] [Object-centric LeJEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02404)
*Jakob Geusen,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种对象级自监督学习方法，通过使用现成的SAM分割掩码来稳定对象表示学习，避免了传统对象级自监督中分割与表示的循环依赖问题，从而在数据效率上优于图像级自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像级自监督方法（如LeJEPA）需要大量训练数据，而对象级表示学习虽然能提高数据效率，但在完全自监督设置中存在循环依赖问题：有意义的分割需要良好的对象表示，而良好的对象表示又需要一致的对象分割。这种不稳定性阻碍了对象级自监督学习的发展。

Method: 1) 使用现成的SAM模型提供对象掩码作为训练输入，避免分割与表示的循环依赖；2) 将LeJEPA框架从图像级扩展到对象级，使用其分布反塌缩目标处理可变大小的对象集合；3) 添加实例分离损失，将同一场景中的其他对象作为负样本来提升表示质量。

Result: 在两种模型规模和10-100% COCO数据比例下，对象级LeJEPA在多个下游任务上优于图像级LeJEPA：在DAVIS上表现更好（跟踪），在ImageNet-1k上（分类），在ADE20k上（分割），在NAVI上（重识别）。方法显示了更好的数据效率和表示质量。

Conclusion: 通过使用现成SAM掩码来稳定训练过程，对象级LeJEPA成功地解决了对象级自监督学习中的循环依赖问题，实现了比图像级方法更好的数据效率和下游任务性能。这种方法为对象中心表示学习提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).

</details>


### [115] [Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02421)
*Anqi Tang,Wenhao Sun,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频率感知语义补偿的无反演图像编辑方法，通过增强生成早期的有效信号来改善全局编辑能力，同时保持背景结构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无反演图像编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在全局属性变换时，编辑轨迹在早期时间步可能无法有效脱离源分布，导致背景结构仅得到适度保留，全局修改有限。原因是高噪声状态下主导的流形追踪流会削弱文本条件方向的影响。

Method: 提出一种无反演、频率感知的语义补偿策略，在生成的早期阶段增强有效信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。该方法不依赖反演过程。

Result: 所提方法在改善全局编辑能力的同时不牺牲背景保真度，提高了文本引导图像编辑的效果。

Conclusion: 通过频率感知的语义补偿机制，可以解决无反演图像编辑框架中早期时间步编辑能力受限的问题，实现了更好的全局属性变换效果和背景保持。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.

</details>


### [116] [Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02425)
*Francesca Pistilli,Simone Alberto Peirone,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-Ego是一个在Ego4D基础上扩展的大规模时空场景图标注集，用于表示人-环境交互的显式、可组合、可编辑的表征。研究者还提出了GLEN模型来对场景图序列进行推理，以及A-GEF任务来预测场景的动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频分析方法往往依赖隐式的视觉或语言对齐表征，忽视了场景动态的结构化推理。研究者认为显式、可组合且可编辑的人-环境交互表征对于丰富的基础活动理解至关重要。

Method: 提出了SG-Ego标注集（在Ego4D基础上添加时空场景图标注），开发了GLEN模型（基于图的模型，在场景图序列上进行操作以对齐文本动作并建模时间演化），并设计了A-GEF任务（活动驱动的图编辑预测问题）。

Result: GLEN模型在多个下游任务上表现出色，包括EgoMCQ、EgoCVR等检索基准，以及EXPLORE-Bench和新提出的A-GEF等长时程推理基准。相比于原始视频基线，GLEN取得强劲结果，在通常由多模态大语言模型处理的推理场景中表现出优势。

Conclusion: 时空场景图以及基于它们的推理模型是视频理解（甚至更广泛领域）的强大可组合和可解释表征形式，能够支持可控和结构化的场景动态预测。

Abstract: Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

</details>


### [117] [MARVEL: Margin-Aware Robust von Mises-Fischer Expert Learning for Long-Tailed Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02435)
*A. S. Anudeep,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种用于医学图像分析的新型OOD检测框架MARVEL，通过非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器、多专家系统和异常值专家组件，在三个医疗数据集上显著改善了异常检测性能，FPR95指标分别降低了8.45%、13.02%和36.90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床部署中，自动化诊断系统面对未见过的病例时需要保持可靠性，但深度模型经常以高置信度错误分类OOD输入。现有研究大多假设平衡数据集，评估的OOD来源粗糙或非临床，缺乏对不同OOD场景的全面评估。

Method: 提出包含三个关键组件的框架：(1)非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器，能学习非线性决策边界；(2)多专家框架，其中边际感知NvMF分类器专门处理标签分布的不同区域以更好应对不平衡；(3)专门训练用于区分内点和异常点数据的异常值专家，增强OOD检测。

Result: 在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTCRC数据集上的评估显示，相比最先进方法分别实现了平均FPR95降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%的显著改进。通过全面的消融研究验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法能够可靠识别不熟悉病例以便转交给临床医生，支持真实工作流程中更安全的AI辅助诊断。该框架在处理医疗数据不平衡和多样化OOD场景方面表现出色。

Abstract: For clinical deployment, it is essential that automated diagnostic systems remain reliable when confronted with previously unseen cases, yet deep models routinely misclassify out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs with high confidence, underscoring the need for more robust OOD detection methods. Although substantial effort has been devoted to improving model robustness, most of the existing literature assumes balanced datasets, evaluates OOD detection on coarse or non-clinical OOD sources, or lacks comprehensive assessment across diverse OOD scenarios. To address the gaps, we propose a novel methodology trained on diverse and imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across a clinically reflective OOD spectrum. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a Nonlinear von Mises-Fisher (NvMF) classifier capable of learning non-linear decision boundaries, with theoretical proof of its asymptotic connection to cosine classifiers; (2) a multi-expert framework in which margin-aware NvMF classifiers specialise in different regions of label distribution to better handle imbalance; and (3) an outlier expert trained explicitly to distinguish inlier from outlier data, thereby strengthening OOD detection. Evaluation on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC datasets demonstrates consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving mean FPR95 reductions of 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively. These gains are further supported by comprehensive ablations that validated the contributions of each component. This enables reliable identification of unfamiliar cases for deferral to clinicians, supporting safer AI-assisted diagnosis in real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL.

</details>


### [118] [OrbitQuant: Data-Agnostic Quantization for Image and Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02461)
*Donghyun Lee,Jitesh Chavan,Duy Nguyen,Sam Huang,Liming Jiang,Priyadarshini Panda,Timo Mertens,Saurabh Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrbitQuant是一种无需数据的权重-激活量化方法，通过随机置换块哈达玛旋转在归一化旋转基中量化，解决了扩散变换器中激活值随时间步长、提示词和引导分支变化的问题，实现从图像到视频的无调优迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器(DiTs)在图像和视频生成方面达到SOTA，但多步采样和参数增长使得推理成本高昂。传统的后训练量化(PTQ)面临挑战，因为DiT激活值会随时间步长、提示词和引导分支变化，导致每次更换检查点或模态都需要重新拟合校准数据。

Method: 提出OrbitQuant，一种数据无关的权重-激活量化器：1）在归一化旋转基中量化，使用随机置换块哈达玛旋转将每个坐标集中在固定的已知边际分布周围；2）对权重行进行离线量化，将旋转吸收到权重中；3）运行时仅保留前向旋转操作。

Result: 在FLUX.1、Z-Image-Turbo、Wan 2.1和CogVideoX等多个模型上，该方法在多个低位设置下达到后训练量化的SOTA性能，并将图像扩散变换器的PTQ推进到W2A4位宽且保持可用的生成质量。无需模态特定调优即可从图像迁移到视频。

Conclusion: OrbitQuant通过旋转基量化策略有效解决了扩散变换器中激活分布动态变化的问题，实现了高效、可迁移的量化解决方案，为扩散模型的部署提供了实用的后训练量化方法。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image and video generation, but their multi-step sampling and growing parameter count make inference expensive. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is the natural remedy, yet DiT activations shift across timesteps, prompts, and guidance branches, forcing prior methods to re-fit calibration data for every new checkpoint or modality. We present OrbitQuant, a data-agnostic weight-activation quantizer that bypasses range estimation by quantizing in a normalized, rotated basis. In this basis, a randomized permuted block-Hadamard (RPBH) rotation concentrates each coordinate around one fixed, known marginal regardless of the input, so a single Lloyd-Max codebook serves all timesteps, prompts, and layers of a given input dimension. We extend the same quantizer to weight rows offline, absorbing the rotation into the weights so that it cancels inside each linear layer and only a forward rotation on the activations remains at runtime. The same recipe transfers from image to video with no per-modality tuning. Across FLUX.1, Z-Image-Turbo, Wan 2.1, and CogVideoX, it sets the state of the art for PTQ at several low-bit settings. It also pushes PTQ of image diffusion transformers to W2A4 with usable generation quality.

</details>


### [119] [Interpretation-Oriented Cloud Removal via Observation-Anchored Residual Flow with Geo-Contextual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02471)
*Ziyao Wang,Maonan Wang,Yucheng He,Xianping Ma,Ziyi Wang,Hongyang Zhang,Yirong Cheng,Man-on Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GACR是一个用于光学遥感云去除的统一框架，通过物理基础的残差反演和语义对齐，既保证了重建的保真度，又提升了下游任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云去除方法往往只关注视觉真实性，忽视了其对下游分析任务的影响，导致语义漂移和下游性能下降。需要开发能够同时保证重建保真度和语义保持的方法。

Method: 提出Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR)框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow)：将云去除重新形式化为物理基础的残差反演过程，将生成轨迹锚定到有云观测而非纯噪声；2) Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA)：利用视觉基础模型引导的语义流形约束重建过程。

Result: 在6个云去除数据集和12个下游任务上的大量实验表明，GACR在重建质量和下游任务准确性方面均表现优异，有效保持了复杂地形的空间-语义完整性。

Conclusion: GACR通过物理基础的生成方法和语义约束，成功解决了云去除中视觉保真与语义保持的平衡问题，为遥感下游任务提供了更可靠的预处理工具。

Abstract: Cloud removal (CR) is essential for optical remote sensing, serving as a prerequisite for reliable downstream interpretation, such as semantic segmentation and change detection. However, existing CR approaches often prioritize visual realism while overlooking their impact on subsequent analytical tasks, leading to semantic drift and degraded downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR), a unified framework that jointly ensures faithful reconstruction and robust interpretability. At its core, GACR incorporates Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow), which reformulates CR as a physically grounded residual inversion process. By anchoring the generative trajectory to the cloudy observation rather than pure noise, OAR-Flow enables fast, stable, and faithful reconstruction. To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.

</details>


### [120] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu,Zizhuo Lin,Jianwen Sun,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EAGLE-360是一个用于360°全景环境主动视觉搜索的框架，通过全局到局部的探索策略解决了标准多模态大语言模型在建模全景属性（如极地畸变和连续拓扑）方面的局限性，显著提升了搜索准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在360°全景环境的主动视觉搜索中存在根本性局限，无法有效建模全景属性（如严重极地畸变和连续圆柱拓扑），导致目标检测准确性显著下降。现有全景搜索方法过度依赖碎片化局部视点，缺乏全局全景先验，导致短视、低效的探索和在目标不可见时难以进行稳健的错误恢复。

Method: 提出EAGLE-360（Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration）框架，利用全局先验建立初始整体视角，通过迭代推理逐步缩小搜索空间，而非进行详尽的局部搜索。在架构上，采用RoPE Rolling（坐标移位位置编码机制）来无缝建模全景的连续拓扑。构建大规模EAGLE-360数据集（包含14,000+张4K全景图和70,000+轮高质量VQA对话）。采用集成监督微调（SFT）和组相对策略优化（GRPO）的训练管道，有效激发复杂的空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，EAGLE-360在360°视觉搜索方面建立了新的最先进水平，相对于基础模型实现了近8倍的准确率提升，同时显著增强了探索效率。

Conclusion: EAGLE-360通过全局到局部的探索范式、RoPE Rolling位置编码机制以及创新的训练方法，成功解决了全景环境中的视觉搜索挑战，为全景视觉理解任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [121] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang,Xuankun Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EADP是一种用于视觉语言模型的熵感知密集剪枝框架，通过统计熵过滤文本噪声并使用子模最大化进行结构化压缩，在严格token预算下保持细粒度视觉线索，实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在密集指令和细粒度查询下无法保留关键线索，主要存在两个瓶颈：1）文本噪声在密集跨模态评分中广泛传播；2）标准token选择方法导致特征碎片化

Method: 提出EADP框架：1）使用统计熵量化和过滤文本噪声，获得稳健的细粒度指令相关性评分；2）将token选择重构为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，而非简单的Top-K选择，确保视觉表示的整体性和非冗余性

Result: 大量实验表明，EADP改善了VLMs的准确性-效率权衡，在严格token预算下稳健地保留了细粒度视觉线索，在挑战性多模态基准测试中实现了SOTA性能

Conclusion: EADP通过结构化压缩方法有效解决了视觉语言模型中的密集剪枝问题，通过熵感知噪声过滤和子模优化，显著提升了在密集指令和细粒度查询下的性能表现

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02486)
*Yejun Zhang,Xinjue Wang,Zihan Wang,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMix是一个无需描述符的视觉定位框架，通过多层次的几何鉴别性增强，显著提升了基于几何匹配的定位精度，缩小了与基于描述符方法的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需描述符的视觉定位虽然具有存储效率高、保护场景隐私和简化地图维护等优势，但其准确性远低于基于描述符的方法。这种差距源于几何匹配中几何鉴别性不足：缺少视觉外观时，现有方法未能充分利用局部几何线索、缺乏关键点间的全局上下文，且过度依赖单一关键点检测器。

Method: GeoMix在三个层次增强几何鉴别性：1) 局部层面：使用方向感知和距离感知的嵌入来丰富邻域聚合，捕捉细粒度空间结构；2) 全局层面：通过可学习的上下文节点聚合和重新分配场景级信息，利用交叉注意力解决局部感受野之外的模糊性；3) 训练层面：利用检测器无关的几何空间进行Mix-Training，跨多个关键点检测器学习表示。

Result: 在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night数据集上的实验表明，GeoMix在无需描述符方法中达到新的最先进水平：将75百分位旋转误差降低了89%，平移误差降低了高达90%，同时能够零样本推广到未见过的检测器，并缩小了与基于描述符管道的差距。

Conclusion: GeoMix通过多层次几何鉴别性增强，显著提升了无需描述符视觉定位的精度，证明了多检测器训练在几何匹配空间中的有效性，为无需描述符方法提供了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.

</details>


### [123] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang,Shuming Hu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS)新任务和PanoSeeker方法，通过记忆增强代理在360°环境中主动搜索并分割指定目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参照分割模型仅处理静态固定视角图像，无法满足具身AI在连续360°环境中进行主动感知的需求

Method: 提出PanoSeeker记忆增强代理，集成视觉语言模型与EgoSphere空间视觉记忆，通过监督微调和强化学习优化搜索轨迹

Result: 在新建立的APRS基准测试中，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上显著优于现有最先进基线方法

Conclusion: APRS任务扩展了参照分割在具身AI中的应用，PanoSeeker通过记忆增强的主动感知机制实现了高效的目标搜索和分割

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [124] [From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02508)
*Dengyang Jiang,Mengmeng Wang,Harry Yang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究发现自流（Self-Flow）方法的改进主要源于噪声维度的数据增强而非不同噪声水平token之间的交互作用，通过引入注意力分离机制证实了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的自对齐方法如SRA和Self-Flow通过构建扩散模型内部的对齐来消除对外部预训练编码器的依赖。然而，从SRA到Self-Flow的改进机制——双时间调度——尚未得到充分研究。Self-Flow将其性能提升归因于不同噪声水平token之间的交互作用，但本文质疑这种解释，探讨改进是否实际上源于噪声维度的数据增强。

Method: 引入注意力分离（Attention Separation）机制，该方法保持与Self-Flow相同的双时间步输入，但阻止分配给不同噪声水平的token之间的注意力交互。通过这种方式分离因素，研究改进的真正来源。进一步将自表示对齐与双时间步和注意力分离增强相结合。

Result: 令人惊讶地发现，移除不同噪声水平token之间的交互并不会降低性能，甚至能提升性能，这表明从SRA到Self-Flow的改进主要源于数据增强而非token交互。注意力分离本身通过将单个图像分割成多个有效训练部分来提供增强效果，从而扩展训练数据。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了自流方法改进的真正机制是噪声维度的数据增强，而非不同噪声水平token之间的交互。注意力分离机制不仅验证了这一发现，还提供了额外的数据增强效果。在ImageNet上的实验证明了这种结合自表示对齐、双时间步和注意力分离增强设计的有效性。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.

</details>


### [125] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu,Rundi Wu,Philipp Henzler,Nikolai Kalischek,Michael Oechsle,Fabian Manhardt,Marc Pollefeys,Andreas Geiger,Federico Tombari,Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个极简主义的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接在原始3D点云图块上操作，超越复杂的基于潜空间的扩散模型，且无需点云tokenizer


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像3D重建方法依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或将几何压缩到潜空间以利用预训练的潜扩散模型。这种架构开销和复杂损失公式被认为是不必要的

Method: 构建一个基于朴素ViT的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接在原始3D点云图块上操作，使用预训练DINOv3的图像token进行条件化。从头训练扩散骨干，无需点云tokenizer

Result: 方法超越复杂的基于潜空间的扩散模型，比混合替代方案简单得多。产生更锐利的几何结构，在高度模糊区域（如透明物体）更鲁棒

Conclusion: 极简的像素空间扩散方法在单图像3D重建任务上优于复杂的潜扩散和混合方法，证明复杂的架构开销和损失函数不是必需的

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>


### [126] [Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02516)
*Qiaowei Miao,Kehan Li,Yawei Luo,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align4D是一个灵活的X-to-4D生成框架，能够将任意模态输入（文本、图像、视频、3D）转换为连贯的视频-3D对，使用视频指导4D运动，3D数据指导4D几何形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成扩散模型在多模态控制下能够合成高质量的图像、视频和3D内容，但任意用户定义的模态到4D（X-to-4D）生成仍然具有挑战性，主要问题包括：构建多样化数据集成本高昂，现有方法可扩展性有限。

Method: Align4D提出了三个关键技术：1) 物体距离对齐：搜索视频对齐和多视角对齐的物体距离，协调4D渲染与视频以及多视角扩散模型先验；2) 运动-几何联合对齐：通过同步的视频和3D输入约束已知和未知视角，确保一致的4D生成；3) 异步优化：解耦高斯属性和变形网络训练，增强运动和几何保真度。

Result: 提出了X4D数据集用于基准测试，该数据集整合了提示、图像、视频和3D数据。在X4D和Consistent4D数据集上的实验表明，Align4D在X-to-4D生成中实现了最先进的质量和一致性。

Conclusion: Align4D是一个有效的X-to-4D生成框架，通过创新的对齐技术和优化策略，解决了任意模态输入到4D内容生成的挑战，在质量和一致性方面达到领先水平。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.

</details>


### [127] [WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02517)
*Hanlin Wang,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Qingyan Bai,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Yixuan Li,Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldDirector是一个高度可控的视频世界模型框架，通过解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成，实现了持久动态对象记忆和无限制视点探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型存在两个主要问题：1) 将物理动态与像素渲染纠缠在一起；2) 依赖连续的视觉观察来维持运动，这限制了模型对长时间消失后重新出现的对象进行精确身份保持的能力。

Method: 框架采用两阶段方法：首先使用大语言模型（LLM）协调3D轨迹与相机运动，然后将这些编排的轨迹作为控制信号输入到视频生成模型中，实现语义运动编排与视觉生成的明确解耦。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法支持合成复杂且持续时间长的事件，具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态对象记忆能力，能够成功保持动态实体的精确视觉身份，即使它们在长时间离开视野后重新进入场景。

Conclusion: WorldDirector框架通过解耦运动逻辑与视觉生成，在视频世界建模中实现了更高的可控性、物理逻辑严格性和外观稳定性，为持久动态对象记忆提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [TokenScope: Token-Level Explainability and Interpretability for Code-Oriented Tasks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01235)
*Amirreza Esmaeili,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenScope是一个用于基于解码器的LLM的交互式可解释性工具，提供代码生成过程中的token级度量和分析功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM在代码生成过程中如何进行token级决策是一个主要挑战，现有工具缺乏解码时信号、细粒度不确定性度量以及探索替代生成路径的交互机制

Method: 开发TokenScope工具，暴露token级度量、注意力模式和结构信息，支持交互式token替换、反事实分支和通过抽象语法树进行代码感知聚合

Result: 通过统一解码时信号与结构程序分析，TokenScope能够系统性研究LLM在代码生成期间的行为

Conclusion: TokenScope为LLM代码生成提供了交互式可解释性和分析框架，增强了研究者对模型决策过程的理解

Abstract: Understanding how Large Language Models (LLMs) make token-level decisions during code generation remains a major challenge for both researchers and practitioners. While recent tools provide insights into model internals or generation outcomes, they often lack decoding-time signals, fine-grained uncertainty measures, and interactive mechanisms for exploring alternative generation paths. We present TokenScope, an interactive interpretability and analysis tool for decoder-based LLMs that exposes token-level metrics, attention patterns, and structural information during generation. TokenScope supports interactive token replacement, counterfactual branching, and code-aware aggregation via abstract syntax trees. By unifying decoding-time signals with structural program analysis, TokenScope enables systematic investigation of LLM behaviour during code generation.

</details>


### [129] [Safeguarding LLM Agents from Misalignment through Provenance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01236)
*Yining She,Yiliang Liang,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProvenanceGuard：一个基于溯源概念框架的多阶段管道，通过追踪代理上下文的可验证证据来检测LLM代理工具调用是否与用户意图对齐，显著降低了误判率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理获得更强大的工具访问权限，确保其行动与用户意图保持一致变得至关重要。现有基于LLM-as-a-judge的运行时护栏缺乏系统性推理框架，导致判断不一致且难以审计，可能造成难以撤销的有害后果

Method: 提出基于溯源分析的概念框架，将不匹配检测形式化为确定提议的工具调用是否有可追踪的上下文证据支持。在此基础上开发ProvenanceGuard多阶段管道，在工具执行前分析代理行动是否存在三种类型的不匹配，仅当行动与用户查询对齐时才允许执行

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench和WorkBench两个基准测试的10个骨干LLM上评估。相比LLM-as-a-judge基线，ProvenanceGuard将不匹配轨迹的错误率从42.9%降至1.8%（Agent-SafetyBench）和从32.1%降至17.3%（WorkBench），同时将任务成功轨迹的干预负担从30.5%降至12.8%，并且在匹配轨迹上没有引入统计显著的额外干预

Conclusion: 结构化、基于溯源的推理为保护LLM代理避免不匹配提供了有效且实用的基础，显著改善了现有方法的判断一致性和可审计性

Abstract: As LLM agents gain increasing access to powerful tools, ensuring that their actions are aligned with the user's intent becomes critical. When an agent's proposed tool invocation deviates from the user's intent -- a phenomenon called misalignment -- it may lead to harmful consequences that are difficult to undo. Existing runtime guardrails rely on an LLM-as-a-judge paradigm that lacks a systematic framework for reasoning about alignment, often producing judgments that are inconsistent or difficult to audit. Motivated by provenance analysis, we propose a provenance-based conceptual framework that formalizes misalignment detection as determining whether a proposed tool call is supported by traceable evidence in the agent's context. Building on this framework, we propose ProvenanceGuard, a multi-stage pipeline that analyzes the agent's action for three types of misalignment before the selected tool is executed and only allows the action to take place when it is considered aligned with the user's input query. We evaluated our proposed approach on two different benchmarks, Agent-SafetyBench and WorkBench, across 10 backbone LLMs. Compared to the LLM-as-a-judge baseline, ProvenanceGuard reduces error rate on misaligned traces from 42.9% to 1.8% on Agent-SafetyBench and from 32.1% to 17.3% on WorkBench, while reducing intervention burden on task-successful traces from 30.5% to 12.8% and introducing no statistically significant increase in unnecessary interventions on aligned traces. These results demonstrate that structured, provenance-based reasoning provides an effective and practical foundation for safeguarding LLM agents from misalignment.

</details>


### [130] [Kara: Efficient Reasoning LLM Serving via Sliding-Window KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01237)
*Shen Han,Yuyang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Kara是一种滑动窗口KV缓存压缩方法，通过双向注意力机制在解码时选择重要KV对，并能将选中的KV对扩展为灵活大小的语义块，结合KvLLM框架有效减少KV缓存内存使用并提高输出吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型推理时生成的长思维链会累积大量KV缓存，导致解码延迟高和吞吐量有限。现有KV缓存压缩方法存在两个主要问题：1)阈值触发的压缩策略可能对吞吐量提升有限甚至降低吞吐量，且可能完全消除序列某些块中的KV对，加剧信息损失；2)通常仅保留孤立的KV对或固定大小的块，无法在任意令牌位置保留重要的灵活大小语义块。

Method: 提出Kara滑动窗口KV缓存压缩方法：1)仅在最近生成的上下文上执行解码时压缩；2)利用双向注意力机制对窗口内的KV对进行评分和选择；3)设计Token2Chunk模块将选中的KV对扩展为语义块；4)将Kara适配到PagedAttention并开发KvLLM推理框架（基于vLLM）。

Result: 大量实验证明Kara和KvLLM在性能上取得一致改进：减少KV缓存内存使用，有效提高输出吞吐量，同时保持模型推理质量。

Conclusion: Kara通过滑动窗口压缩和灵活语义块保留机制，解决了现有KV缓存压缩方法的局限性，KvLLM框架进一步优化了推理效率，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning language models often generate long chain-of-thought (CoT), which accumulates a massive KV cache during the decoding phase and incurs high decoding latency and limited throughput. To address these issues, KV cache compression has emerged as a promising technique for reducing memory overhead by selectively removing unimportant KV pairs while preserving useful ones for subsequent decoding. Nevertheless, we identify two key limitations in existing KV cache compression methods: 1) their threshold-triggered compression policy may provide limited throughput improvement or even reduce throughput, and may fully eliminate KV pairs from certain blocks of the sequence, potentially worsening information loss. 2) they typically retain either isolated KV pairs or fixed-size chunks with rigid boundaries, failing to preserve important flexible-sized chunks at arbitrary token positions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Kara, a sliding-window KV cache compression method that performs decoding-time compression by operating only on the recently generated context. Kara leverages bidirectional attention to score and select informative KV pairs in the window. To enable flexible preservation of important semantic information, we design a Token2Chunk module to expand a subset of selected KV pairs into chunks. Furthermore, we adapt Kara to PagedAttention and develop KvLLM, an inference framework built upon vLLM, which reduces KV cache memory usage and effectively improves output throughput. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent performance improvements of proposed Kara and KvLLM.

</details>


### [131] [SPARCLE: SPeaker-aware Aligned Representations via Contrastive Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01238)
*Priyam Mazumdar,Yurii Halychanskyi,Steven Guo,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPARCLE是一种说话人感知的字符表示模型，通过对比学习将字符与其精确的声学实现对齐，在低资源语音合成任务中显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音合成从音素表示转向直接字符建模。虽然音素解决了文本到声学的一对多映射问题，但它们依赖的字素到音素(G2P)系统无法捕捉说话人特定的声学变化。尽管大规模环境下字符模型优于音素模型，但在低资源设置中表现不佳。

Method: 提出SPARCLE模型，通过对比学习目标将字符与对应的Wav2Vec2声学表示对齐，同时以说话人身份为条件。该模型训练时丰富字符及其精确的声学实现，可作为下游文本到语音(TTS)任务中G2P系统的替代方案。

Result: SPARCLE显著提高了语音合成质量，在极端低资源设置下，相比标准字符模型将词错误率降低了一半。这表明说话人感知的字符表示在低资源环境中特别有效。

Conclusion: SPARCLE通过结合说话人身份信息和对齐字符与声学表示，成功解决了低资源语音合成中字符模型的性能瓶颈，为无需G2P系统的端到端TTS系统提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech synthesis have shifted from phoneme representations to direct grapheme modeling. While phonemes address the one-to-many mapping between text and acoustics, they rely on grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) systems that fail to capture speaker-specific acoustic variation. Prior work demonstrates that grapheme-based models outperform phoneme-based systems at scale, but not in low-resource settings.
  In this paper, we propose SPARCLE, a speaker-aware grapheme representation model that enriches characters with their precise acoustic realizations. SPARCLE is trained with a contrastive objective to align graphemes with corresponding Wav2Vec2 acoustic representations while conditioned on speaker identity. The resulting model serves as a replacement to G2P systems for downstream text-to-speech (TTS) tasks. We demonstrate that SPARCLE improves generation quality, reducing word error rates by half in extreme low-resource settings compared to standard grapheme-based models.

</details>


### [132] [Breaking Safety at the Token Boundary: How BPE Tokenization Creates Exploitable Gaps in LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01239)
*Tung-Ling Li,Hongliang Liu,Yuhao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现字符级扰动通过BPE分词将安全关键词拆分为子词片段，绕过现代LLM的安全对齐，因为现有对齐数据集不包含故意分片输入，导致模型无法识别此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型虽经过安全对齐，但仍存在被字符级扰动攻击的漏洞。作者观察到现有安全对齐数据集（HarmBench等）缺乏故意将安全关键词汇拆分为BPE子词片段的对抗样本，这可能导致模型无法有效识别和防御此类攻击。

Method: 研究采用端到端测试方法，在五个模型家族上进行评估：1）通过优化目标使安全标记分片化来测试拒绝触发反转；2）使用激活修补技术定位信号中断层；3）对齐数据扫描验证分片缺失；4）针对性变异实验隔离安全词的作用；5）防御方面测试不同DPO配置的训练效果，并引入Conv-Benign诊断工具区分选择性修复与全局崩溃。

Result: 攻击成功率显著：优化安全标记分片可反转80-100%拒绝触发，其中48%产生真正有害输出（各模型29-65%）。激活修补显示信号中断发生在最后约30%层。对齐数据扫描发现30,000个示例中无分片提示。防御方面，所有DPO配置均无法稳定关闭攻击成功率，SFT训练在分片提示上虽能在3/5模型中关闭攻击成功率，但导致对良性提示的拒绝率也升高。

Conclusion: BPE分词中的安全词汇分片是模型安全对齐的关键漏洞。现有对齐数据缺乏故意分片输入导致防御不足。修复此问题需要专门训练，但现有方法（如LoRA-16）难以在不引起全局崩溃的情况下实现选择性修复。需要更精细的训练策略和诊断工具来有效防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Character-level perturbations bypass safety alignment in modern LLMs despite leaving prompts human-readable. We identify and test a central structural mechanism: BPE tokenization fragments safety-critical words into sub-word pieces, and the three public alignment datasets we surveyed contain no intentionally fragmented inputs. The mechanism is a chain, tested end-to-end on five model families (Qwen-3-4B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Gemma-3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B). An optimization targeting safety-token fragmentation flips the first-token refusal trigger on 80-100% of refused HarmBench prompts, with 48% of those flips producing genuinely harmful outputs (per-model 29-65%; gap-vs-behavior ROC-AUC 0.66-0.98, pooled 0.84). Activation patching localizes the disrupted signal to the last ${\sim}30\%$ of layers; an alignment-data scan finds zero fragmented prompts among 30,000 examples (positive-control recall $\geq 99\%$ at attack-relevant intensities); and targeted-mutation experiments isolate safety words as the disruption locus. On the defense side, a 68-cell grid (55 trained checkpoints) shows that no DPO configuration achieves seed- and pool-stable ASR closure on the three families with closed pool-size confounds. SFT trained on fragmented prompts closes ASR on 3/5 families but only via global collapse that raises refusal on benign prompts as well, indicating the missing distribution is necessary but not sufficient under the LoRA-16 recipe we tested. To distinguish selective repair from global collapse, we introduce Conv-Benign, a candidate paired diagnostic. All ASR claims are 3-judge-calibrated (cell rankings stable across judges; absolute levels $\pm$18pp; see App.~B.13).

</details>


### [133] [Prompt Framing Distorts Count-Based Evaluation of LLM Error Detection: Evidence from Numeric Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01240)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，基于计数的F1分数在LLM错误检测评估中存在"F1膨胀"问题：F1分数显著提高并不对应实际的跨度定位能力提升。论文引入了ErrorBench协议来量化这种偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM错误检测评估普遍使用基于计数的F1分数作为质量指标，但这种方法可能产生误导性的结果。计数F1的提高可能只是表面现象，并不反映模型在实际错误定位能力上的真实改进。

Method: 提出了ErrorBench，一种控制性压力测试协议，用于评估提示诱导的计数扭曲。使用CoNLL-2014数据集（4,290个响应来自143个段落），在五种提示条件下评估六个当代LLM。采用M2式评分和严格的ERRANT 3.0.0管道进行多参考评分，比较盲提示与锚定提示下的性能差异。

Result: 研究发现显著的F1膨胀现象：锚定提示下F1膨胀高达0.79分（M2评分）和0.96分（严格匹配）。盲提示到锚定提示的转换使计数F1平均提高+0.21，而多参考ERRANT F0.5仅提高+0.04。高指令依从性的GPT/Claude系统产生更大的计数响应，而Gemini系列产生较小的响应。

Conclusion: LLM校对和文档审查评估应避免使用预填充的错误计数，应在报告基于计数的指标时同时报告跨度感知指标。当前基于计数的评估方法需要重新审视，以确保评估结果能真实反映模型的错误定位能力。

Abstract: Count-based F1 is widely used as a proxy for LLM error-detection quality, but this paper shows that it can rise dramatically without a corresponding improvement in span localization, a gap termed F1 Inflation. The paper introduces ErrorBench, a controlled stress-test protocol for prompt-induced count distortion. ErrorBench evaluates six contemporary LLMs under five prompt conditions over 4,290 responses from 143 CoNLL-2014 passages. Under CoNLL-2014 M2-style scoring, anchored prompts produce up to 0.79 points of F1 Inflation, and up to 0.96 under strict matching. A 100-passage replication using the official ERRANT 3.0.0 pipeline and multi-reference scoring reproduces the pattern: averaged over six models, the Blind-to-Anchored prompt shift raises Count-F1 by +0.21 while raising multi-reference ERRANT F0.5 by only +0.04. The study finds larger count responses in highly instruction-compliant GPT/Claude systems and smaller responses in the Gemini family under this stress-test protocol. The findings suggest that LLM proofreading and document-review evaluations should avoid pre-populated error counts and should report span-aware metrics alongside count-based metrics.

</details>


### [134] [Mapping Text to Multiplex Graph: Prompt Compression as Lévy Walk-Guided Graph Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01241)
*Yaxin Gao,Yao Lu,Jinhong Deng,Jiaqi Nie,Zhe Tang,Jian Zhang,Zhaowei Zhu,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGP是一种将提示压缩建模为冗余感知图剪枝的方法，通过在多层图上联合建模细粒度注意力依赖和粗粒度语义关系，使用Levy游走平衡局部利用和全局探索，显著提升了压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示压缩方法将文本视为扁平标记序列，未能捕捉重要信息的分布式特性。重要信息通常分布在多个位置，通过局部句法依赖和全局语义关系相互连接。这种关系结构自然地表示为图，其中标记或句子成为节点，依赖关系成为边。

Method: RAGP将提示压缩建模为多层图上的冗余感知图剪枝。该方法构建一个联合建模细粒度注意力依赖（基于注意力机制）和粗粒度语义关系的多层图。为了在异质结构中高效识别非冗余节点（密集局部子图和稀疏全局连接），采用Levy游走算法，其重尾步长分布自然平衡局部利用和全局探索。

Result: 在LongBench基准测试中，RAGP在4倍压缩比下获得平均分数49.3，优于现有基于LLM的压缩方法（如LongLLMLingua在3倍压缩比下获得48.8）。此外，RAGP在多个任务上也超越了最先进的基于视觉的文本压缩范式。

Conclusion: RAGP通过图结构建模提示中的复杂依赖关系，结合Levy游走算法有效处理异质图结构，为提示压缩提供了新颖且高效的解决方案，在保持语义完整性的同时实现了更好的压缩性能。

Abstract: Existing prompt compression methods treat text as flat token sequences, failing to capture the distributed nature of important information, which is often spread across multiple locations and connected through both local syntactic dependencies and global semantic relations. Such relational structure is naturally represented as a graph, where tokens or sentences become nodes and their dependencies become edges. To this end, we propose RAGP, which formulates prompt compression as Redundancy-Aware Graph Pruning on a multiplex graph that jointly models fine-grained attention-based dependencies and coarse-grained semantic relations. To efficiently identify non-redundant nodes in this heterogeneous structure (dense local subgraphs and sparse global connections), we employ Levy walks whose heavy-tailed step distribution naturally balances local exploitation with global exploration. Experiments on LongBench show that RAGP achieves an average score of 49.3 under a 4x compression ratio, outperforming existing LLM-based compression methods, such as LongLLMLingua, which attains 48.8 at a 3x compression ratio. Besides, RAGP also surpasses state-of-the-art vision-based text compression paradigms on multiple tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RAGP-B0CB.

</details>


### [135] [Office Comprehension Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01245)
*Firoz Shaik,Mateus Picanço Lima Gomes,Tanvir Aumi,Jingci Wang,Milos Milunovic,Filip Basara,Ivana Jovanovic,Vishwas Suryanarayanan,Neha Nandan Kenkare,Weiyao Xie,Zhipeng Han,Zheng Zhang,Waleed Shahid,Jay Rathi,Russell Scherer,Thong Q. Nguyen,Michael Bentley,Tamara Stankovic,Rasika Chakravarthy,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OCB是首个联合评估LLM系统对Word、Excel、PowerPoint原生文件格式及其变体理解的公共基准，包含文件保真度问答和领域问答两个轨道，评估结构视觉感知和专业领域推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能够联合评估LLM系统对Office文档（.docx、.xlsx、.pptx）全面理解的公共基准，特别是对文档结构、视觉元素和专业领域知识的多维度综合评估。

Method: OCB包含两个轨道：1) 文件保真度问答，测试对表格、图表、嵌入图像、公式及特定应用元素的结构和视觉感知；2) 领域问答，基于12个专业领域的真实行业文档，测试需要多步骤分析和文档间综合的专家级推理。采用原子化、可二进制评分的声明分解，由LLM评审团独立评分。

Result: 即使在默认推理模式下最强的前沿系统在领域问答中仅达到约59.3%的准确率；在同一层级内增加思考深度对性能提升不明显，而升级到更高级别的产品层级仅带来适度改进。

Conclusion: OCB填补了Office文档理解评估的空白，为LLM系统在实际办公应用中的能力提供了标准化的测评框架，揭示了当前系统在处理复杂Office文档理解任务时的局限性。

Abstract: We introduce Office Comprehension Bench (OCB), the first public benchmark to jointly evaluate LLM systems on Word, Excel, and PowerPoint comprehension over native file formats (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx) and their variants. OCB consists of two tracks. File Fidelity Q&A tests structural and visual perception of office artifacts - tables, charts, embedded images, formulas, and app-specific elements such as headers, speaker notes, and named ranges. Domain Q&A tests expert-level reasoning grounded in real-world industry documents across 12 professional domains, with queries requiring multi-step analysis and synthesis across documents. Each reference answer is decomposed into atomic, binary-gradable claims, and an ensemble of LLM judges scores responses against each claim independently. Even the strongest frontier system in its default reasoning mode reaches only about 59.3% on Domain Q&A; increasing thinking depth within a tier does not move performance materially, while moving to a higher product tier yields modest gains. We release the dataset, evaluation tooling, judge prompt, and a public leaderboard.

</details>


### [136] [RuleChef: Grounding LLM Task Knowledge in Human-Editable Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01293)
*Ádám Kovács,Nadia Verdha,Gábor Recski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RuleChef是一个使用大语言模型生成NLP任务可执行规则的框架，通过任务描述和标注示例生成规则，然后基于额外示例和人类反馈进行迭代改进，最终产生快速、确定且可检查的规则系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习模型在NLP任务中通常是黑箱且计算密集，需要可解释、确定性强且计算效率高的解决方案。现有方法缺乏同时具备人类可理解性、确定性执行和基于少量数据高效生成规则的能力。

Method: 使用大语言模型基于任务描述和标注示例生成初始规则，然后通过迭代改进机制：基于额外示例和人类反馈对现有规则进行修补。LLMs仅在训练时使用，用于合成规则并根据验证集上的失败进行迭代修补。还可以通过观察现有模型的输入输出对来引导规则生成。

Result: 初步评估在文本分类和命名实体识别任务上进行，展示了RuleChef的有效性。生成的规则系统具有速度快、确定性执行和可检查性的特点。框架已作为开源软件以Apache 2.0协议发布。

Conclusion: RuleChef提供了一个创新框架，能够利用LLMs的强大能力生成高质量的可执行规则，同时避免了将LLMs部署到生产环境中的计算成本和不确定性，为构建可解释、高效的NLP系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: We present RuleChef, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to generate executable rules for NLP tasks such as text classification, Named Entity Recognition (NER), or relation extraction. Rules are generated based on a task description and a set of labeled examples, then they are iteratively improved based both on additional examples and on human feedback overexisting rules. RuleChef can also be used to bootstrap rules using the observed input-output pairs from any existing model for a given task. LLMs are used only at learning time, synthesizing rules and iteratively patching them based on failures measured on a held-out split. The result of this process is a fast, deterministic, and inspectable rule system. Preliminary evaluation is performed on both classification and NER tasks. We release RuleChef as open-source software under an Apache 2.0

</details>


### [137] [TurnNat: Automatic Evaluation of Turn-Taking Naturalness in Dyadic Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01345)
*Hao Zhang,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velazquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnNat框架，通过因果性预测模型评估双通道语音对话中的话轮转换自然度，基于未来语音活动状态的负对数似然测量时序异常性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的话轮转换自然度评估方法存在局限性：要么依赖人工判断，要么使用特定行为的时序指标，难以在统一框架下比较异质性的时序失败情况。需要一种自动化的评估框架来全面评估话轮转换的自然度。

Method: 提出TurnNat框架，基于自然对话训练的因果性话轮转换预测模型，用于估计未来双说话者的语音活动状态。通过观测到的未来活动的负对数似然(NLL)来测量时序异常性。将帧级NLL汇总到从话语起始和偏移提取的话轮转换边界单元(TBU)上，并将TBU的平均值和尾部得分聚合为对话级自然度得分。

Result: 构建了一个包含自然对话和扰动对话片段配对的受控扰动基准数据集，并通过人类自然度判断进行了验证。实验结果表明，TurnNat成功识别了各种异质性时序失败引起的非自然话轮转换扰动。

Conclusion: TurnNat提供了一个统一的、自动化的框架来评估话轮转换的自然度，能够有效识别各种类型的时序异常，为全双工语音对话系统的自然度评估提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Turn-taking naturalness is central to full-duplex spoken dialogue systems, yet its automatic evaluation remains limited. Existing evaluations often rely on human judgments or behavior-specific timing metrics, making it difficult to compare heterogeneous timing failures within a unified framework. We propose TurnNat, a likelihood-based framework for automatic turn-taking naturalness evaluation in two-channel spoken dialogue. A causal turn-taking prediction model trained on natural conversations estimates future two-speaker voice-activity states, and the negative log-likelihood (NLL) of the observed future activity measures timing atypicality. TurnNat pools frame-level NLLs over turn-taking boundary units (TBUs) extracted from utterance onsets and offsets, and aggregates mean and tail TBU scores into a dialogue-level naturalness score. We further construct a controlled perturbation benchmark of paired natural and perturbed dialogue clips, validated by human naturalness judgments. Experiments on this benchmark show that TurnNat successfully identifies unnatural turn-taking perturbations across heterogeneous timing failures.

</details>


### [138] [RusFinChain: A Russian Benchmark for Verifiable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Finance with Fuzzy-Aligned Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01388)
*M. K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RusFinChain是第一个俄语符号推理基准，用于金融领域的可验证思维链评估，包含5,280个参数化示例和增强的评估指标，揭示了大语言模型在推理步骤与最终答案之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融推理基准缺乏中间推理步骤的监督：FINCHAIN仅限于英语，FINESSE-Bench虽然有俄语部分但依赖多项选择题且缺乏步骤级监督。需要专门针对俄语金融领域的可验证思维链评估基准来推动俄语金融AI发展。

Method: 1) 构建RusFinChain数据集，涵盖17个领域、172个主题，包含5,280个从可执行Python模板生成的参数化示例；2) 每个示例提供带中间数值的金标准推理链用于自动验证；3) 引入增强指标：模糊数值对齐和软注意力对齐；4) 在分层样本上评估8个开源大语言模型，生成8,100个响应。

Result: 模型在步骤对齐方面达到约0.65的Hard F1分数，但仅有约29%的最终答案正确，揭示显著的推理差距。新提出的模糊和软指标与最终答案正确性的相关性更强（Spearman rho约0.48），优于原始ChainEval指标（rho约0.38-0.46），展示了更好的诊断能力。

Conclusion: RusFinChain填补了俄语金融可验证推理基准的空白，提出的增强指标能更准确地诊断模型推理能力。数据、代码和评估框架的发布将促进俄语社区可验证金融AI的发展。

Abstract: Multi-step symbolic reasoning is essential for robust financial analysis, yet most benchmarks neglect intermediate reasoning steps. FINCHAIN introduced verifiable Chain-of-Thought (CoT) evaluation but is limited to English. FINESSE-Bench includes a Russian block but relies on multiple-choice questions without step-level supervision. We present RusFinChain, the first Russian-language symbolic benchmark for verifiable CoT reasoning in finance. It spans 17 domains, 172 topics, and comprises 5,280 parameterized examples from executable Python templates, ensuring contamination-free evaluation. Each example includes a gold-standard reasoning chain with intermediate numeric values for automatic verification. We also introduce enhanced metrics: Fuzzy Numeric Alignment and Soft-Attention Alignment. We evaluate 8 open-weight LLMs on a stratified sample, generating 8,100 responses. Results reveal a substantial reasoning gap: models achieve Hard F1 of ~0.65 for step alignment, but only ~29% of final answers are correct. Our fuzzy and soft metrics show stronger correlation with final-answer correctness (Spearman rho approx 0.48) than the original ChainEval (rho approx 0.38-0.46), demonstrating superior diagnostic power. We release dataset, code, and evaluation framework to foster verifiable financial AI for the Russian-speaking community.

</details>


### [139] [Multi-Objective Exploration and Preference Optimization via Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01392)
*Hongyan Xie,Yikun Ban,Ruiyu Fang,Zixuang Huang,Deqing Wang,Jianxin Li,Shuangyong Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MI-EPO：一个基于互信息的信息论框架，通过最大化生成响应、偏好反馈和偏好向量之间的联合条件互信息，统一多目标探索和对齐，提高响应与偏好向量的对齐度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过偏好向量训练策略并使用在线直接偏好优化，但探索不确定性会导致不同偏好向量下生成的响应奖励分布重叠，生成的响应可能无法有效对齐相应的偏好向量。

Method: 提出MI-EPO框架，最大化生成响应、偏好反馈和偏好向量之间的联合条件互信息。采用概率路由机制，自然分解目标对齐和偏好感知探索，鼓励模型生成可区分且对齐不同偏好条件的响应。

Result: 在安全对齐和辅助任务上的实验表明，MI-EPO显著提高了生成响应与偏好向量的对齐度，使输出更可控，并在多个目标间实现了稳定的权衡。

Conclusion: MI-EPO通过信息论框架有效解决了多目标对齐中响应与偏好向量对齐不足的问题，实现了更好的可控性和多目标权衡。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with diverse and heterogeneous human values requires multi-objective alignment methods to effectively trade off conflicting preference dimensions. Current methods achieve this trade-off by training policies conditioned on preference vectors and leveraging online direct preference optimization. However, exploration uncertainty can cause the reward distributions of responses generated under different preference vectors to overlap, and the generated responses may fail to effectively align with the corresponding preference vectors. In this paper, we propose Multi-Objective Exploration and Preference Optimization via Mutual Information (MI-EPO), an information-theoretic framework. It unifies multi-objective exploration and alignment by maximizing the joint conditional mutual information among generated responses, preference feedback, and preference vectors. By incorporating a probabilistic routing mechanism, MI-EPO naturally decomposes objective alignment and preference-aware exploration, encouraging the model to generate responses that are distinguishable and aligned with different preference conditions. Experiments on safe alignment and helpful assistant tasks show that MI-EPO significantly improves the alignment between generated responses and preference vectors, makes the outputs more controllable, and achieves stable trade-offs across multiple objectives.

</details>


### [140] [MultAttnAttrib: Training-Free Multimodal Attribution in Long Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01420)
*Dang Quang Thien Tran,Quang V. Dang,Vinamra Tyagi,Sai Soorya Rao Veeravalli,Trang Nguyen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Nedim Lipka,Koustava Goswami,Samyadeep Basu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了MultAttnAttrib——一种无训练的多模态归因生成方法，利用模型预填充、注意力头和校准阈值定位证据；同时引入了MultAttrEval基准数据集，用于评估长文档中的多模态归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于事实的QA系统在AI助手中的应用越来越广泛，准确归因生成的答案对用户信任和模型安全至关重要。虽然单模态归因已被深入研究，但多模态环境下的归因仍然相对缺乏研究。

Method: MultAttnAttrib是一种无需训练的方法，利用模型的预填充传递、选择的注意力头和校准阈值来定位文档中的源证据。无需额外训练，直接在预训练模型基础上工作。

Result: 实验结果表明MultAttnAttrib在各种归因生成方法中表现一致优越，包括几种基于提示的方法，并与GPT 5.4等最新前沿模型相匹配。方法显著提高了单模态和多模态归因类型的准确性，同时归因延迟仅为相同基础模型直接推断的七分之一。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多模态归因研究的空白，提出的无训练方法在保持高效性的同时实现了优秀的归因性能，为基于事实的多模态QA系统提供了有效的归因解决方案。

Abstract: As grounded QA systems are increasingly deployed in AI assistants, accurately attributing generated answers to evidence is critical for user trust and model safety. While unimodal attributions have been explored in depth, the multimodal setting remains relatively under-researched. As a result, we introduce MultAttnAttrib, a training-free attribution-generation method that leverages a model's prefill pass, selected attention heads, and calibrated thresholds to locate source evidence within a document.
